# Soft UI Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Move every screen of Conidolly from Calma suiza to Soft UI v6 (gris neutro, relief means touch, one sign control with a two-tap path, iOS text size end to end) without changing any state, action, server logic, database or Trigger.dev task.

**Architecture:** Lote 1 swaps the tokens in `app/globals.css` (old names kept as aliases), fixes `cn`, adds the large-text switch and the brand literals. Lote 2 rebuilds the UI kit in `components/ui` (new `estado`, `bloque`, `panel`, `grupo`, `mas-opciones`, `volver`, `icono`; one sign control; a sticky sign bar; a portalled toast) with Testing Library tests first. Lotes 3 to 6 rewrite the screens on that kit with pure, unit-tested presentation helpers (`faltaParaFirmar`, `faltaParaFirmarVideo`, `resaltarFrases`, `cifraDeRevision`, the Inicio modelo); lote 7 adds the large-text and contrast rules, removes the aliases, regenerates the baselines and closes with docs, verification, an impeccable critique and audit, and a Vercel-only deploy runbook.

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16 App Router (Server and Client Components), Tailwind v4 (`@theme inline`, `tema-cerrado.css`), `tailwind-merge` 3 (`extendTailwindMerge`), `class-variance-authority`, Phosphor icons, Vitest 4 + Testing Library + jsdom, Playwright 1.59 (fake services, visual snapshots, axe), Biome 2.2, pnpm.

---

## Amendment (2026-10-16): accessibility and snapshot hardening

The repo already has `@axe-core/playwright` (`tests/e2e/accesibilidad.spec.ts`) and stable snapshot defaults
(`playwright.config.ts`: `animations: "disabled"`, `caret: "hide"`, `maxDiffPixelRatio: 0.01`; `visual.spec.ts`
emulates `reducedMotion: "reduce"`). Two additions, no new dependency:

1. **Axe per new state (from Lote 2 on).** Every task that adds or restyles a screen state also adds that state to
   `tests/e2e/accesibilidad.spec.ts` with `new AxeBuilder({ page }).withTags(["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21aa",
   "wcag22aa"]).analyze()` and `expect(violations).toEqual([])`, opening menus, "Más opciones" folds and toasts before
   analyzing. Lote 7 (Task 45 onwards) runs the same list a second time with large text (`data-letra="grande"` and the
   root font size at 200 %). A state with a known, accepted exception uses `.exclude(selector)` with a comment saying why.
2. **Masks for moving data (Lote 1, with the token task).** In `tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts`, every `toHaveScreenshot`
   call passes `mask` for elements that change between runs (dates relative to today, times, durations counting down,
   balances), via a shared `data-snapshot="varia"` attribute on those elements, and `maskColor` set to the `--honda`
   value so masks blend in. Baselines are generated and compared on macOS only (the existing setup); the runbook
   states that a CI on another OS must regenerate them first.


**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conidolly-soft-ui-design.md` (read it whole first; its decision numbers are quoted in every task).
**Visual source of truth:** `~/Projects/conidolly-rediseno/soft-ui/v6/` (`soft.css`, 14 HTML screens, `*-completa.png` and `*-200-completa.png` renders).
**Style reference:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-voz-en-la-puerta-1.md`.

**Rules (every task):**

- Work in a git worktree on a new branch `feat/soft-ui` from `main` (superpowers:using-git-worktrees, folder
  `.worktrees/soft-ui`). Note: `pnpm lint` run from the main checkout fails today with "Found a nested root
  configuration" because `.worktrees/voz-en-la-puerta-1/biome.json` exists; inside the new worktree it runs normally.
- Strict TDD where behaviour exists: write the failing test, run it and see it red, then the minimum code, then green.
  Pure style changes are verified with the visual snapshots (procedure below).
- Scope (spec decision 1): only `app/globals.css`, `components/**`, the JSX of `app/**` screens, client UI state, copy,
  and the pure presentation helpers `lib/inicio/modelo.ts`, `lineaDeFirma` in `lib/dominio/saldos.ts`,
  `lib/ajustes/presentar.ts`, `lib/diseno/*`, `app/(app)/guion/[id]/textos.ts` (plus the new pure files this plan
  creates next to the screens). Never touch `lib/dominio/estados.ts`, actions, `lib/produccion/**`,
  `lib/publicacion/**`, data readers, migrations or `trigger/**`. A screen never gains a database read.
- `"use client"` stays on line 1 of client modules. After every `pnpm check`, run
  `grep -L '^"use client"' $(grep -rl '"use client"' app components)` and expect no output.
- Biome safe mode only (`pnpm check`); never `--unsafe`.
- No em dashes anywhere in code or copy: before each commit, `git diff --cached | grep '—'` prints nothing.
- No `#000`, `#fff`, no hex colour in `app/` or `components/` (tokens only; `app/icon.tsx` and `app/apple-icon.tsx`
  read `lib/diseno/marca.ts`). No `as any`, `@ts-expect-error`, non-null assertions or silent catches.
- Copy for Coni: Spanish of Chile, `tú`. Every new string is in the spec's copy table (decision 17) unless a task says
  otherwise and the note at the end lists it.
- One commit per task. Message in English, imperative, then a blank line, then
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Do not push and do not deploy (the last task is a runbook
  that needs Federico's explicit OK).
- Every task leaves `pnpm test:run`, `pnpm type` and `pnpm build` green. The E2E specs and visual baselines a task
  affects are updated in that same task.

**Commands:**

- `pnpm type` (next typegen + tsc), `pnpm build`
- `pnpm test:run` (all unit) and `pnpm test:run tests/unit/<file>` (one file)
- `pnpm db:start` (local Supabase; `colima start` first if Docker is not up), `pnpm db:reset && pnpm test:db`
- `pnpm test:e2e` and `pnpm test:e2e tests/e2e/<file>` (builds the app and starts the fake services itself)
- `pnpm lint`: known reports today are `tests/unit/duracion.test.ts` (useTemplate) and the unused suppression in
  `components/reproductor-video.tsx` (Task 19 removes it). Nothing new may appear.

**Visual snapshot procedure** (named in the tasks as "the visual procedure for <test names>"; the commands are
repeated in each task):

1. `pnpm test:e2e tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts -g "<test names>"`: expected FAIL on the screens the task changed. The
   expected, actual and diff images land in `playwright-report/`.
2. `pnpm exec playwright show-report`: open each failed test, look at the diff, and compare the actual image with the
   v6 render named in the task (`~/Projects/conidolly-rediseno/soft-ui/v6/<n>-<name>-completa.png`). The diff must
   only show what the task changed; anything else goes back to the code.
3. `pnpm test:e2e tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts -g "<test names>" --update-snapshots` and stage
   `tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts-snapshots/` with the task's commit.

When a task changes every screen, step 1 runs the whole file (no `-g`). Playwright matches `-g` against
"<project> <file> <title>", so an exact test name is anchored with a leading space and `$` (`-g " ajustes$"`), never `^`.

## Lote 1: Tokens

### Task 1 (Lote 1): `cn` keeps the custom sizes

Today `twMerge("text-meta font-medium text-tinta")` returns `"font-medium text-tinta"`: every `cn` that mixes a custom
size with a colour drops the size (spec, facts from the code, and decision 2).

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/utils.test.ts`
- Modify: `lib/utils.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
// tests/unit/utils.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

describe("cn", () => {
  it("keeps a custom text size next to a text colour", () => {
    expect(cn("text-meta", "text-apagado")).toBe("text-meta text-apagado")
    expect(cn("text-meta font-medium text-tinta")).toBe("text-meta font-medium text-tinta")
  })

  it("lets a later custom size replace an earlier one", () => {
    expect(cn("text-meta", "text-cuerpo")).toBe("text-cuerpo")
    expect(cn("text-boton", "text-insignia")).toBe("text-insignia")
  })

  it("merges the relief shadows and the radii as one group each", () => {
    expect(cn("shadow-relieve", "active:shadow-hundido", "shadow-relieve-alto")).toBe(
      "active:shadow-hundido shadow-relieve-alto",
    )
    expect(cn("rounded-control", "rounded-panel")).toBe("rounded-panel")
  })

  it("treats the rem sizing names as spacing", () => {
    expect(cn("min-h-boton", "min-h-objetivo")).toBe("min-h-objetivo")
    expect(cn("size-casilla", "size-foto")).toBe("size-foto")
    expect(cn("px-[22px] min-h-boton w-full text-boton", "px-[18px] min-h-objetivo text-cuerpo", "px-0 w-auto")).toBe(
      "min-h-objetivo text-cuerpo px-0 w-auto",
    )
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail**

Run: `pnpm test:run tests/unit/utils.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL. The first test receives `"text-apagado"` and `"font-medium text-tinta"`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

```ts
// lib/utils.ts
import { type ClassValue, clsx } from "clsx"
import { extendTailwindMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

// The custom scale of app/globals.css. Without it tailwind-merge reads `text-meta` as a colour and drops it next to
// `text-tinta` (spec 2026-10-16, decision 2). Keep these lists in step with the @theme block of globals.css.
const fusionar = extendTailwindMerge({
  extend: {
    theme: {
      text: ["pantalla", "pieza", "guion", "boton", "cuerpo", "meta", "insignia", "marca"],
      shadow: [
        "relieve",
        "relieve-alto",
        "relieve-fijo",
        "relieve-alto-fijo",
        "hundido",
        "relieve-pulgar",
        "relieve-perilla",
        "hundido-marcado",
      ],
      radius: ["control", "panel", "bloque", "barra", "toast", "casilla", "video"],
      spacing: [
        "objetivo",
        "boton",
        "fila",
        "fila-baja",
        "casilla",
        "pildora",
        "play",
        "nav",
        "foto",
        "riel",
        "interruptor-ancho",
        "interruptor-alto",
        "punto",
        "punto-chico",
      ],
    },
  },
})

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return fusionar(clsx(inputs))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run and see green**

Run: `pnpm test:run tests/unit/utils.test.ts`
Expected: PASS. Then `pnpm test:run` (all unit) and `pnpm type`: green. No visual change yet (the names do not exist in
the CSS until Task 3; the existing classes merge as before or better).

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add lib/utils.ts tests/unit/utils.test.ts
git commit -m "Keep custom text sizes, shadows and radii when merging classes

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

### Task 2 (Lote 1): The large-text predicate and its head script

Decision 6: the app switches the layout when fewer than 18 rem fit across the screen. The predicate is unit-tested and
the script that runs before the first paint inlines the same arithmetic.

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/diseno/letra.ts`
- Create: `tests/unit/letra.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
// tests/unit/letra.test.ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { ANCHO_MINIMO_EN_REM, esLetraGrande, SCRIPT_DE_LETRA } from "@/lib/diseno/letra"

function anchoDeVentana(px: number) {
  Object.defineProperty(window, "innerWidth", { value: px, configurable: true, writable: true })
}

afterEach(() => {
  document.documentElement.removeAttribute("data-letra")
  document.documentElement.style.fontSize = ""
})

describe("esLetraGrande", () => {
  it("switches below 18 rem across", () => {
    expect(ANCHO_MINIMO_EN_REM).toBe(18)
    expect(esLetraGrande(390, 17)).toBe(false)
    expect(esLetraGrande(390, 23)).toBe(true)
    expect(esLetraGrande(375, 21)).toBe(true)
  })

  it("never switches on a root it cannot read", () => {
    expect(esLetraGrande(390, 0)).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe("SCRIPT_DE_LETRA", () => {
  it("marks the root with data-letra=grande on the same arithmetic, and clears it when the text gets smaller", () => {
    anchoDeVentana(390)
    document.documentElement.style.fontSize = "23px"
    new Function(SCRIPT_DE_LETRA)()
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.letra).toBe("grande")

    document.documentElement.style.fontSize = "17px"
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event("resize"))
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.letra).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it("checks again when the app comes back to the screen", () => {
    anchoDeVentana(390)
    document.documentElement.style.fontSize = "17px"
    new Function(SCRIPT_DE_LETRA)()
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.letra).toBeUndefined()

    document.documentElement.style.fontSize = "24px"
    document.dispatchEvent(new Event("visibilitychange"))
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.letra).toBe("grande")
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail**

Run: `pnpm test:run tests/unit/letra.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL with `Failed to resolve import "@/lib/diseno/letra"`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

```ts
// lib/diseno/letra.ts
/** Fewer rem than this across the screen and the pinned bars would cover half of it (spec decision 6). */
export const ANCHO_MINIMO_EN_REM = 18

/** At 390 px this is true from a 21.7 px root up (xxxLarge and the accessibility sizes); at 375 px from 20.8 px. */
export function esLetraGrande(anchoPx: number, raizPx: number): boolean {
  if (raizPx <= 0) return false
  return anchoPx / raizPx < ANCHO_MINIMO_EN_REM
}

/**
 * Runs in <head> before the first paint: the arithmetic of `esLetraGrande`, inlined because no module loads that early.
 * iOS applies a new text size when the app comes back to the screen, hence pageshow and visibilitychange.
 */
export const SCRIPT_DE_LETRA = `(function(){var d=document.documentElement;function medir(){var r=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(d).fontSize);if(r>0&&innerWidth/r<${ANCHO_MINIMO_EN_REM})d.setAttribute("data-letra","grande");else d.removeAttribute("data-letra")}medir();addEventListener("resize",medir);addEventListener("pageshow",medir);document.addEventListener("visibilitychange",medir)})()`
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run and see green**

Run: `pnpm test:run tests/unit/letra.test.ts`
Expected: PASS (4 tests). Then `pnpm type`: clean.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add lib/diseno/letra.ts tests/unit/letra.test.ts
git commit -m "Add the large-text predicate and the head script that applies it

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

### Task 3 (Lote 1): Gris neutro tokens, iOS body type and the brand literals

Decision 2 (tokens, aliases), 3 (type), 7 (focus, contrast, forced colours) and 15 (brand literals). The old names
stay as aliases of the new values so every screen keeps compiling and renders with the new palette and the old layout.
The home-screen icon letter moves to `COLOR_DE_LUZ` here (spec lists the icons in lote 6; `lib/diseno/marca.ts`
changes in this task and the icons read it, so they move together).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Replace `tests/unit/diseno-reglas.test.ts` with:

```ts
// @vitest-environment node
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs"
import { join, relative } from "node:path"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"

const RAIZ = join(__dirname, "..", "..")

function archivos(carpeta: string, extensiones: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const salida: string[] = []
  for (const nombre of readdirSync(join(RAIZ, carpeta))) {
    const ruta = join(carpeta, nombre)
    if (statSync(join(RAIZ, ruta)).isDirectory()) salida.push(...archivos(ruta, extensiones))
    else if (extensiones.some((ext) => nombre.endsWith(ext))) salida.push(ruta)
  }
  return salida
}

const CODIGO = [...archivos("app", [".tsx", ".ts"]), ...archivos("components", [".tsx", ".ts"])]
const leer = (ruta: string) => readFileSync(join(RAIZ, ruta), "utf-8")
const CSS = leer("app/globals.css")

// Files that must carry literal colours because the OS reads them before any CSS (marca.ts feeds them).
const CON_COLOR_LITERAL = new Set(["app/icon.tsx", "app/apple-icon.tsx"])

function luminancia(hex: string): number {
  const canal = (inicio: number) => {
    const c = Number.parseInt(hex.slice(inicio, inicio + 2), 16) / 255
    return c <= 0.03928 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4
  }
  return 0.2126 * canal(1) + 0.7152 * canal(3) + 0.0722 * canal(5)
}

function contraste(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [mas, menos] = [luminancia(a), luminancia(b)].sort((x, y) => y - x)
  return ((mas ?? 0) + 0.05) / ((menos ?? 0) + 0.05)
}

/** Hue in degrees, 0 to 360. */
function tono(hex: string): number {
  const [r, g, b] = [1, 3, 5].map((i) => Number.parseInt(hex.slice(i, i + 2), 16) / 255) as [number, number, number]
  const max = Math.max(r, g, b)
  const delta = max - Math.min(r, g, b)
  if (delta === 0) return 0
  const base = max === r ? ((g - b) / delta) % 6 : max === g ? (b - r) / delta + 2 : (r - g) / delta + 4
  return (base * 60 + 360) % 360
}

/** The value a token is declared with (the first declaration, :root), following one `var(--x)` alias. */
function valor(nombre: string): string {
  const declarado = new RegExp(`--${nombre}: ([^;]+);`).exec(CSS)?.[1]?.trim() ?? ""
  const alias = /^var\(--([a-z-]+)\)$/.exec(declarado)?.[1]
  return alias ? valor(alias) : declarado
}

describe("design rules", () => {
  it("has no hex colour literal in app/ or components/", () => {
    const culpables = CODIGO.filter((ruta) => !CON_COLOR_LITERAL.has(ruta)).filter((ruta) =>
      /["'`\s(]#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\b/.test(leer(ruta)),
    )
    expect(culpables).toEqual([])
  })

  it("has no em dash in app/ or components/", () => {
    expect(CODIGO.filter((ruta) => leer(ruta).includes("—"))).toEqual([])
  })

  it("keeps 'use client' as the very first line of every client module", () => {
    const culpables = CODIGO.filter((ruta) => leer(ruta).includes('"use client"')).filter(
      (ruta) => !leer(ruta).startsWith('"use client"'),
    )
    expect(culpables).toEqual([])
  })

  it("defines the gris neutro palette of Soft UI v6", () => {
    const paleta = [
      ["piedra", "#ebebea"],
      ["piedra-alta", "#f4f4f3"],
      ["piedra-honda", "#dcdcdb"],
      ["luz", "#fbfbfa"],
      ["tinta", "#262626"],
      ["apagado", "#5a5a59"],
      ["campo", "#727271"],
      ["hilo", "#dcdcdb"],
      ["error", "#b5137a"],
      ["firma", "#a8285a"],
      ["sobre-firma", "#fbfbfa"],
    ] as const
    for (const [token, hex] of paleta) expect(valor(token), token).toBe(hex)
  })

  it("is light only: no dark block and no system colour-scheme switch", () => {
    expect(CSS).toContain("color-scheme: light")
    expect(CSS).not.toContain('data-theme="dark"')
    expect(CSS).not.toContain("prefers-color-scheme")
  })

  it("keeps error apart from the signature, and every text pair at AA on the three stones", () => {
    expect(valor("error")).not.toBe(valor("firma"))
    for (const superficie of ["piedra", "piedra-alta", "piedra-honda"]) {
      for (const tinta of ["tinta", "apagado", "error"]) {
        expect(contraste(valor(tinta), valor(superficie)), `${tinta} on ${superficie}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5)
      }
      expect(contraste(valor("campo"), valor(superficie)), `campo on ${superficie}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
    }
    expect(contraste(valor("luz"), valor("tinta"))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5)
    expect(contraste(valor("luz"), valor("firma"))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5)
    expect(contraste(valor("firma"), valor("piedra-honda"))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
    expect(tono(valor("firma"))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(320)
    expect(tono(valor("firma"))).toBeLessThanOrEqual(350)
  })

  it("draws the focus ring in ink, never in the signature", () => {
    expect(CSS).toMatch(/:focus-visible \{\s*outline: 2px solid var\(--tinta\);\s*outline-offset: 2px;/)
    expect(CSS).not.toMatch(/--anillo-foco: [^;]*firma/)
  })

  it("sets the type on the iOS body size, only on touch screens, with px spacing", () => {
    expect(CSS).toMatch(/html \{\s*font-size: 106\.25%;/)
    expect(CSS).toMatch(
      /@media \(hover: none\) and \(pointer: coarse\) \{\s*@supports \(font: -apple-system-body\) \{\s*html \{\s*font: -apple-system-body;/,
    )
    expect(CSS).toContain("--spacing: 4px;")
  })

  it("draws icons with Phosphor only, through the SSR entry in server modules", () => {
    expect(CODIGO.filter((ruta) => leer(ruta).includes("lucide-react"))).toEqual([])
    const servidorConIconoCliente = CODIGO.filter(
      (ruta) => !leer(ruta).startsWith('"use client"') && /from "@phosphor-icons\/react"/.test(leer(ruta)),
    )
    expect(servidorConIconoCliente).toEqual([])
  })

  it("uses no token name retired before Calma suiza", () => {
    const viejos =
      /\b(acento|ok-tinta|ok-fondo|aviso-fondo|error-fondo|aviso-tinta|error-tinta|pilar-tinte|pilar-tinta|pilar-punto|font-serif|text-display|text-title|text-script|text-body|text-label|rounded-(sm|md|lg|xl)|ease-fuerte)\b/
    expect(CODIGO.filter((ruta) => viejos.test(leer(ruta)))).toEqual([])
  })

  it("keeps the signature colour to the slider, the committed date, the wordmark dot and the public page", () => {
    const permitidos = new Set([
      "components/deslizar-para-aprobar.tsx",
      "app/(app)/guion/[id]/pantalla-guion.tsx",
      "components/ui/wordmark.tsx",
      "app/l/page.tsx",
    ])
    const culpables = CODIGO.filter(
      (ruta) => /\b(text|bg|fill|border|ring|outline)-firma\b/.test(leer(ruta)) && !permitidos.has(ruta),
    )
    expect(culpables).toEqual([])
  })

  it("lists the files it scans (sanity: the walk found code)", () => {
    expect(CODIGO.map((ruta) => relative(".", ruta)).length).toBeGreaterThan(50)
  })
})
```

```css
@import "tailwindcss";
/* Closes the default palette, radii, shadows and type sizes (before the tokens below define the real ones). */
@import "./tema-cerrado.css";

/*
 * Soft UI v6, gris neutro (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conidolly-soft-ui-design.md, decision 2; the values come
 * from ~/Projects/conidolly-rediseno/soft-ui/v6/soft.css). This is the only place with literal colours: components use
 * the names below (bg-piedra, text-tinta, shadow-relieve, ...). Light only.
 */
:root {
  color-scheme: light;

  /* ---------- Colour ---------- */
  --piedra: #ebebea; /* page and every raised surface */
  --piedra-alta: #f4f4f3; /* reading panels, flat groups. Tinta 13.75:1 */
  --piedra-honda: #dcdcdb; /* sunken tracks and fields, status pills, warning and error blocks, disabled. Tinta 11.03:1 */
  --luz: #fbfbfa; /* light side of the relief; text on tinta (14.62:1), firma (6.51:1) and video (11.27:1) */
  --tinta: #262626; /* all text, primary fill, checked boxes, progress, focus ring. 12.69:1 on piedra */
  --apagado: #5a5a59; /* meta, help, disabled labels. 5.79, 6.27 and 5.03:1 on piedra, alta and honda */
  --campo: #727271; /* empty checkbox and radio rings, high-contrast outlines. 3.51:1 on honda */
  --hilo: var(--piedra-honda); /* 1 px dividers between rows, decorative */
  --error: #b5137a; /* magenta fresa: error titles and the destructive confirm, always after an icon. 4.58:1 on honda */
  --firma: #a8285a; /* raspberry: the thumb, its trail, the wordmark dot, /l. 4.91:1 against its honda track */
  --sobre-firma: var(--luz);
  --firma-rastro: rgb(168 40 90 / 0.14); /* trail while dragging or armed */
  --video: #3a3836; /* the video frame before it loads */
  --velo-video: rgb(38 38 38 / 0.78); /* badges and controls over the video */
  --riel-video: rgb(251 251 250 / 0.35); /* the part of the video rail still to play */

  /* ---------- Relief: sunken, raised and high, never a fourth level ---------- */
  --relieve: -6px -6px 14px rgb(251 251 250 / 0.9), 6px 6px 14px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.18);
  --relieve-alto: -10px -10px 24px rgb(251 251 250 / 0.9), 10px 10px 24px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.22);
  --relieve-fijo: 0 -4px 14px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.1), inset 0 1px 0 rgb(251 251 250 / 0.9);
  --relieve-alto-fijo: 0 -8px 24px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.16), inset 0 1px 0 rgb(251 251 250 / 0.9);
  --hundido: inset 4px 4px 10px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.18), inset -4px -4px 10px rgb(251 251 250 / 0.9);
  --relieve-pulgar: -3px -3px 8px rgb(251 251 250 / 0.7), 3px 3px 8px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.3);
  --relieve-perilla: -2px -2px 5px rgb(251 251 250 / 0.9), 2px 2px 5px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.25);
  --hundido-marcado: inset 2px 2px 5px rgb(60 60 60 / 0.18);

  /* ---------- Type: rem on the iOS body size, so the system text size scales text and targets together ---------- */
  --letra: var(--font-geist-sans), ui-sans-serif, -apple-system, system-ui, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
  --t-pantalla: 2rem; /* screen title */
  --i-pantalla: 1.125;
  --tr-pantalla: -0.022em;
  --t-pieza: 1.5rem; /* piece title, idea hook, "Grabo tu video en 8 s" */
  --i-pieza: 1.167;
  --tr-pieza: -0.014em;
  --t-guion: 1.18rem; /* the script */
  --i-guion: 1.6;
  --t-boton: 1.125rem; /* labels of the 3.25rem buttons and the working line */
  --i-boton: 1.333;
  --t-cuerpo: 1rem; /* body, row titles, small buttons, toast, track label */
  --i-cuerpo: 1.333;
  --i-prosa: 1.55; /* paragraphs: the LinkedIn text, the caption */
  --t-meta: 0.9375rem; /* meta, section labels, pills, help, figures */
  --i-meta: 1.333;
  --t-insignia: 0.8125rem; /* badges on the video */
  --i-insignia: 1.3;
  --t-marca: 2.75rem; /* only the name on /l */
  --i-marca: 1.09;
  --peso-titulo: 600;

  /* ---------- Shape ---------- */
  --r-control: 999px; /* everything touchable */
  --r-panel: 24px;
  --r-bloque: 18px;
  --r-barra: 28px;
  --r-toast: 22px;
  --r-casilla: 9px;
  --r-video: 22px;

  /* ---------- Space: px on the 4/8 grid. Targets and min-heights are rem tokens of the @theme block ---------- */
  --e-1: 4px;
  --e-2: 8px;
  --e-3: 12px;
  --e-4: 16px;
  --e-6: 24px;
  --e-8: 32px;
  --e-10: 40px;
  --e-12: 48px;
  --e-16: 64px;
  --margen: 20px;
  --final-pagina: 96px; /* free space after the last control of a screen without a sign bar */

  /* ---------- The sign control and the bottom nav, in rem; large text shrinks the thumb (decision 6) ---------- */
  --pulgar-firma: 3rem;
  --pista-firma: 3.5rem;
  --hueco-etiqueta: 1rem;
  --alto-nav: calc(3.5rem + 32px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));

  /* ---------- Motion: only in response to touch ---------- */
  --dur-corta: 150ms; /* press: relief to sunken */
  --dur-media: 200ms; /* state change, disclosure, toast */
  --dur-larga: 220ms; /* thumb travelling or springing back */
  --curva-salida: cubic-bezier(0.23, 1, 0.32, 1);
  --curva-vuelta: cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1);
  --latido: 1.6s; /* the working dot and the skeletons */

  /* ---------- Layers ---------- */
  --capa-barra: 10;
  --capa-toast: 20;
}

/* Reduced motion keeps every state change and drops the movement (decision 7). */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  :root {
    --dur-corta: 0ms;
    --dur-media: 0ms;
    --dur-larga: 0ms;
  }
}

/* ---------- Calma suiza names, aliases of the new values while the screens move over (spec "Migration order",
   step 1). Task 48 deletes this block and the one below, and the guard test bans the names. ---------- */
:root {
  --fondo: var(--piedra);
  --superficie: var(--piedra);
  --linea: var(--hilo);
  --pista: var(--piedra-honda);
  --principal: var(--tinta);
  --sobre-principal: var(--luz);
  --sobre-tinta: var(--luz);
  --exito: var(--tinta);
  --aviso: var(--tinta);
  --sobre-video: var(--luz);
  --sombra-barra: var(--relieve-alto-fijo);
  --sombra-flotante: var(--relieve-alto);
  --anillo-foco: 0 0 0 2px var(--piedra), 0 0 0 4px var(--tinta);
  --barra-max: 176px;
  --r-12: 12px;
  --foto-brillo: 1;
}

@theme inline {
  --color-fondo: var(--fondo);
  --color-superficie: var(--superficie);
  --color-linea: var(--linea);
  --color-pista: var(--pista);
  --color-principal: var(--principal);
  --color-sobre-principal: var(--sobre-principal);
  --color-sobre-tinta: var(--sobre-tinta);
  --color-exito: var(--exito);
  --color-aviso: var(--aviso);
  --color-sobre-video: var(--sobre-video);
  --radius-12: var(--r-12);
  --shadow-barra: var(--sombra-barra);
  --shadow-flotante: var(--sombra-flotante);
}
/* ---------- End of the Calma suiza aliases ---------- */

@theme inline {
  --color-transparent: transparent;
  --color-current: currentColor;
  --color-piedra: var(--piedra);
  --color-piedra-alta: var(--piedra-alta);
  --color-piedra-honda: var(--piedra-honda);
  --color-luz: var(--luz);
  --color-tinta: var(--tinta);
  --color-apagado: var(--apagado);
  --color-campo: var(--campo);
  --color-hilo: var(--hilo);
  --color-error: var(--error);
  --color-firma: var(--firma);
  --color-sobre-firma: var(--sobre-firma);
  --color-firma-rastro: var(--firma-rastro);
  --color-video: var(--video);
  --color-velo-video: var(--velo-video);

  --font-sans: var(--font-geist-sans), ui-sans-serif, -apple-system, system-ui, sans-serif;

  --text-pantalla: var(--t-pantalla);
  --text-pantalla--line-height: var(--i-pantalla);
  --text-pantalla--letter-spacing: var(--tr-pantalla);
  --text-pantalla--font-weight: var(--peso-titulo);
  --text-pieza: var(--t-pieza);
  --text-pieza--line-height: var(--i-pieza);
  --text-pieza--letter-spacing: var(--tr-pieza);
  --text-pieza--font-weight: var(--peso-titulo);
  --text-guion: var(--t-guion);
  --text-guion--line-height: var(--i-guion);
  --text-boton: var(--t-boton);
  --text-boton--line-height: var(--i-boton);
  --text-cuerpo: var(--t-cuerpo);
  --text-cuerpo--line-height: var(--i-cuerpo);
  --text-meta: var(--t-meta);
  --text-meta--line-height: var(--i-meta);
  --text-insignia: var(--t-insignia);
  --text-insignia--line-height: var(--i-insignia);
  --text-marca: var(--t-marca);
  --text-marca--line-height: var(--i-marca);
  --leading-prosa: var(--i-prosa);

  --radius-control: var(--r-control);
  --radius-panel: var(--r-panel);
  --radius-bloque: var(--r-bloque);
  --radius-barra: var(--r-barra);
  --radius-toast: var(--r-toast);
  --radius-casilla: var(--r-casilla);
  --radius-video: var(--r-video);

  --shadow-relieve: var(--relieve);
  --shadow-relieve-alto: var(--relieve-alto);
  --shadow-relieve-fijo: var(--relieve-fijo);
  --shadow-relieve-alto-fijo: var(--relieve-alto-fijo);
  --shadow-hundido: var(--hundido);
  --shadow-relieve-pulgar: var(--relieve-pulgar);
  --shadow-relieve-perilla: var(--relieve-perilla);
  --shadow-hundido-marcado: var(--hundido-marcado);

  /* Paddings stay px when the root grows with the iOS text size; targets are rem (lib/utils.ts lists these names). */
  --spacing: 4px;
  --spacing-objetivo: 2.75rem;
  --spacing-boton: 3.25rem;
  --spacing-fila: 4.5rem;
  --spacing-fila-baja: 3.75rem;
  --spacing-casilla: 1.65rem;
  --spacing-pildora: 2rem;
  --spacing-play: 3.5rem;
  --spacing-nav: 3.5rem;
  --spacing-foto: 2.25rem;
  --spacing-riel: 0.625rem;
  --spacing-interruptor-ancho: 3.25rem;
  --spacing-interruptor-alto: 2rem;
  --spacing-punto: 0.625rem;
  --spacing-punto-chico: 0.5rem;

  --ease-salida: var(--curva-salida);
  --ease-vuelta: var(--curva-vuelta);
}

/* 17 px everywhere; iPhone and iPad with touch take Dynamic Type. The media guard is ours: on macOS Safari
   -apple-system-body resolves to the desktop body size and would shrink the app. */
html {
  font-size: 106.25%;
  -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%;
}
@media (hover: none) and (pointer: coarse) {
  @supports (font: -apple-system-body) {
    html {
      font: -apple-system-body;
    }
  }
}

/* The shorthand above also sets a system family: the body sets family, size and line height again. */
body {
  background: var(--piedra);
  color: var(--tinta);
  font-family: var(--letra);
  font-size: var(--t-cuerpo);
  line-height: var(--i-cuerpo);
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
}

/* In the base layer so a utility can change the colour where the surface is dark (the toast, /l). */
@layer base {
  :focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--tinta);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }
  /* Rows that wrap a hidden input show the ring on the row. */
  label:has(> input:focus-visible) {
    outline: 2px solid var(--tinta);
    outline-offset: 2px;
    border-radius: var(--r-bloque);
  }
}

/* Decision 7: with more contrast every raised, sunken or flat container gets a campo outline and apagado turns ink. */
@media (prefers-contrast: more) {
  :root {
    --apagado: var(--tinta);
  }
  :where(
      .shadow-relieve,
      .shadow-relieve-alto,
      .shadow-relieve-fijo,
      .shadow-relieve-alto-fijo,
      .shadow-hundido,
      .shadow-relieve-pulgar,
      [data-contorno]
    ) {
    outline: 2px solid var(--campo);
    outline-offset: -2px;
  }
  :where(
      .shadow-relieve,
      .shadow-relieve-alto,
      .shadow-relieve-fijo,
      .shadow-relieve-alto-fijo,
      .shadow-hundido,
      .shadow-relieve-pulgar,
      [data-contorno]
    ):focus-visible {
    outline-color: var(--tinta);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }
}

/* Shadows vanish in forced colours: raised and sunken shapes get a system border. */
@media (forced-colors: active) {
  :where(
      .shadow-relieve,
      .shadow-relieve-alto,
      .shadow-relieve-fijo,
      .shadow-relieve-alto-fijo,
      .shadow-hundido,
      .shadow-relieve-pulgar,
      .shadow-relieve-perilla,
      .shadow-hundido-marcado,
      [data-contorno]
    ) {
    border: 1px solid CanvasText;
  }
}

/* The toast slides in 8 px and fades; a transition (not a keyframe) so a second toast retargets smoothly. */
.aviso {
  transition:
    transform var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida),
    opacity var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida);
}
@starting-style {
  .aviso {
    transform: translateY(8px);
    opacity: 0;
  }
}

/* An in-line confirmation rises 8 px and fades in, like the toast. */
.confirmacion {
  transition:
    transform var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida),
    opacity var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida);
}
@starting-style {
  .confirmacion {
    transform: translateY(8px);
    opacity: 0;
  }
}

/* Loading: a slow pulse of opacity, never a spinner in the middle of the content. */
@keyframes latido {
  50% {
    opacity: 0.55;
  }
}
.esqueleto {
  animation: latido var(--latido) ease-in-out infinite;
  background: var(--piedra-honda);
  border-radius: var(--r-control);
}

/* The dot that says something real is happening. */
@keyframes trabajando {
  50% {
    opacity: 0.25;
  }
}
.punto-trabajando {
  animation: trabajando var(--latido) ease-in-out infinite;
}

/* A new card of the idea bank slides up 8 px and fades in; a transition on mount, so a quick second tap retargets it. */
.carta-idea {
  transition:
    transform var(--dur-larga) var(--curva-salida),
    opacity var(--dur-larga) var(--curva-salida);
}
@starting-style {
  .carta-idea {
    transform: translateY(8px);
    opacity: 0;
  }
}

/* Reduced motion keeps the opacity and colour changes that explain a state and drops the movement. */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  html {
    scroll-behavior: auto;
  }
  .esqueleto,
  .punto-trabajando {
    animation: none;
  }
  @starting-style {
    .aviso,
    .confirmacion,
    .carta-idea {
      transform: none;
    }
  }
}

/* Fields sink (decision 4): a 3.25rem pill for one line; a text area has 24 px corners and grows with its text. In the
   components layer so a utility can still adjust one. */
@layer components {
  .campo {
    background: var(--piedra-honda);
    box-shadow: var(--hundido);
    border: 0;
    border-radius: var(--r-control);
    padding: 0 20px;
    min-height: 3.25rem;
    color: var(--tinta);
  }
  /* Only a text area grows with its text: a one-line input that did would push its column wider than the screen. */
  textarea.campo {
    field-sizing: content;
    border-radius: var(--r-panel);
    padding: 14px 20px;
  }
  .campo[aria-invalid="true"] {
    outline: 2px solid var(--error);
    outline-offset: 0;
  }
  .campo:disabled {
    box-shadow: none;
    color: var(--apagado);
  }
  .campo::placeholder {
    color: var(--apagado);
  }
}

/* A seekable rail (decision 4): a 0.625rem sunken track, the ink progress (--p) and a raised 1.25rem knob, on a
   2.75rem target. */
.avance {
  --avance-color: var(--tinta);
  --avance-pista: var(--piedra-honda);
  --avance-sombra: var(--hundido);
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  appearance: none;
  width: 100%;
  height: 2.75rem;
  margin: 0;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: transparent;
}
.avance:disabled {
  cursor: default;
  --avance-color: var(--apagado);
}
/* Over the video: a light fill on a translucent rail, no relief (the frame is not a surface). */
.avance.sobre-video {
  --avance-color: var(--luz);
  --avance-pista: var(--riel-video);
  --avance-sombra: none;
}
.avance::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
  height: 0.625rem;
  border-radius: var(--r-control);
  background:
    linear-gradient(var(--avance-color), var(--avance-color)) 0 0 / var(--p, 0%) 100% no-repeat,
    var(--avance-pista);
  box-shadow: var(--avance-sombra);
}
.avance::-webkit-slider-thumb {
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  width: 1.25rem;
  height: 1.25rem;
  margin-top: -0.3125rem;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: var(--r-control);
  background: var(--piedra);
  box-shadow: var(--relieve-perilla);
}
.avance::-moz-range-track {
  height: 0.625rem;
  border-radius: var(--r-control);
  background: var(--avance-pista);
  box-shadow: var(--avance-sombra);
}
.avance::-moz-range-progress {
  height: 0.625rem;
  border-radius: var(--r-control);
  background: var(--avance-color);
}
.avance::-moz-range-thumb {
  width: 1.25rem;
  height: 1.25rem;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: var(--r-control);
  background: var(--piedra);
  box-shadow: var(--relieve-perilla);
}

/* The public page of the bio link is signature-coloured edge to edge, overscroll included. */
body:has([data-superficie="marca"]) {
  background: var(--firma);
}
```

```tsx
      </RootLayout>,
    )
    const cabeza = html.slice(html.indexOf("<head>"), html.indexOf("</head>"))
    expect(cabeza).toContain(SCRIPT_DE_LETRA)
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail**

Run: `pnpm test:run tests/unit/layout.test.tsx`
Expected: FAIL on `expect(cabeza).toContain(SCRIPT_DE_LETRA)`: the rendered head carries no script yet.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

```tsx
// app/layout.tsx
import { GeistSans } from "geist/font/sans"
import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next"
import { SCRIPT_DE_LETRA } from "@/lib/diseno/letra"
import { COLOR_DE_FONDO, NOMBRE_DE_LA_APP } from "@/lib/diseno/marca"
import "./globals.css"

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: NOMBRE_DE_LA_APP,
  description: "Donde Coni aprueba los guiones y los videos de su avatar antes de que salgan al aire.",
  applicationName: NOMBRE_DE_LA_APP,
  appleWebApp: { capable: true, title: NOMBRE_DE_LA_APP, statusBarStyle: "default" },
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
}

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  width: "device-width",
  initialScale: 1,
  viewportFit: "cover",
  themeColor: COLOR_DE_FONDO,
  colorScheme: "light",
}

export default function RootLayout({ children }: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>) {
  return (
    // The head script sets data-letra before React hydrates: that attribute is not React's to compare.
    <html lang="es-CL" className={GeistSans.variable} suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        {/* biome-ignore lint/security/noDangerouslySetInnerHtml: a constant of lib/diseno/letra.ts, no input reaches it. */}
        <script dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: SCRIPT_DE_LETRA }} />
      </head>
      <body className="min-h-dvh">{children}</body>
    </html>
  )
}
```

## Lote 2: UI components

### Task 5 (Lote 2): Icons in `em`, through one helper

Decision 3: Phosphor icons scale with the text (v6: 16 -> 1em, 20 -> 1.2em, 24 -> 1.45em) through a size helper in
`components/ui/icono.ts` rather than per-file numbers. This task adds the helper and moves every numeric `size` to it
in one mechanical pass, so every later task already writes `size={ICONO.medio}`.

**Files:**
- Create: `components/ui/icono.ts`, `tests/unit/icono.test.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/diseno-reglas.test.ts` (one test)
- Modify: every file of `app/` and `components/` with `size={14|16|20|24|28|32}` (37 files today, listed by the grep
  of Step 4)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```ts

      mini: "0.85em",
```

```ts
  })
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail**

Run: `pnpm test:run tests/unit/icono.test.ts tests/unit/diseno-reglas.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL: the import of `@/components/ui/icono` does not resolve, and the new rule lists 37 files.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helper**

```ts
// components/ui/icono.ts
// Not a client module: server and client components size their Phosphor icons with it.
/**
 * Phosphor sizes in em, so an icon grows with the text it sits next to (spec decision 3; v6: 16 -> 1em, 20 -> 1.2em,
 * 24 -> 1.45em). The comment on each is the px size it replaces at the 16 px design root.
 */
export const ICONO = {
  mini: "0.85em", // 14: the check inside a box
  chico: "1em", // 16: next to meta text
  medio: "1.2em", // 20: next to body text, chevrons
  grande: "1.45em", // 24: nav, the sign thumb
  amplio: "1.6em", // 28: the video play
  cierre: "1.9em", // 32: the closing moment
} as const
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Move every numeric size to the helper (mechanical)**

```bash
for f in $(grep -rlE 'size=\{(14|16|20|24|28|32)\}' app components); do
  perl -0pi -e 's/size=\{14\}/size={ICONO.mini}/g; s/size=\{16\}/size={ICONO.chico}/g; s/size=\{20\}/size={ICONO.medio}/g; s/size=\{24\}/size={ICONO.grande}/g; s/size=\{28\}/size={ICONO.amplio}/g; s/size=\{32\}/size={ICONO.cierre}/g' "$f"
  perl -0pi -e 's/\A((?:"use client"\n\n)?)/$1import { ICONO } from "\@\/components\/ui\/icono"\n/' "$f"
done
pnpm check
grep -L '^"use client"' $(grep -rl '"use client"' app components)
grep -rnE 'size=\{[0-9]+\}' app components
```

Expected: the two greps print nothing. `pnpm check` sorts the new import into place.

- [ ] **Step 5: Run and see green**

Run: `pnpm test:run tests/unit/icono.test.ts tests/unit/diseno-reglas.test.ts`
Expected: PASS. Then `pnpm test:run`, `pnpm type`, `pnpm build`: green.

```tsx
})
```

```ts
// components/ui/clases-de-boton.ts
// Not a client module: Server Components use these classes too, for links that look like buttons.
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const PLANO_SI_ESTA_DESHABILITADO =
  "aria-disabled:bg-piedra-honda aria-disabled:text-apagado aria-disabled:shadow-none aria-disabled:active:shadow-none"

/**
 * Decision 4: everything touchable is a raised pill, and pressing sinks it (no scale). One primary per view, label on
 * the left, icon on the right. The destructive variant is only the confirm inside a confirmation.
 */
const variantesDeBoton = cva(
  [
    "inline-flex items-center gap-3 rounded-control text-left font-semibold",
    "transition-[box-shadow,opacity] duration-[var(--dur-corta)] ease-salida",
    "aria-disabled:cursor-not-allowed",
  ],
  {
    variants: {
      variante: {
        primario: `justify-between bg-tinta text-luz shadow-relieve active:shadow-hundido ${PLANO_SI_ESTA_DESHABILITADO}`,
        secundario: `justify-between bg-piedra text-tinta shadow-relieve active:shadow-hundido ${PLANO_SI_ESTA_DESHABILITADO}`,
        callado:
          "justify-start text-tinta underline decoration-1 underline-offset-4 active:opacity-70 aria-disabled:text-apagado aria-disabled:no-underline",
        destructiva: `justify-start bg-piedra text-error shadow-relieve active:shadow-hundido ${PLANO_SI_ESTA_DESHABILITADO}`,
      },
      tamano: {
        grande: "min-h-boton w-full px-[22px] text-boton",
        chico: "min-h-objetivo px-[18px] text-cuerpo",
      },
    },
    // The quiet action has no surface at any size: its target is the 2.75rem row of its text.
    compoundVariants: [{ variante: "callado", class: "min-h-objetivo w-auto px-0 text-cuerpo" }],
    defaultVariants: { variante: "primario", tamano: "grande" },
  },
)

export type OpcionesDeBoton = VariantProps<typeof variantesDeBoton>

/** The classes merged with `cn`, so the compound variant wins over the size it overrides. */
export function clasesDeBoton(opciones: OpcionesDeBoton & { className?: string } = {}): string {
  return cn(variantesDeBoton(opciones))
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/boton.tsx
"use client"

import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, MouseEvent, ReactNode, Ref } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { clasesDeBoton, type OpcionesDeBoton } from "./clases-de-boton"
import { PuntoTrabajando } from "./punto-trabajando"

interface PropiedadesDeBoton extends Omit<ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, "disabled">, OpcionesDeBoton {
  /** Looks and reads as disabled but stays focusable, so a screen reader can find it and read why. */
  deshabilitado?: boolean
  cargando?: boolean
  /** The icon that says what happens next, on the right of the label. Replaced by the working dot while loading. */
  icono?: ReactNode
  /** What the action costs, on the right in place of the icon (v6 `.btn .precio`). */
  precio?: string
  ref?: Ref<HTMLButtonElement>
}

export function Boton({
  variante,
  tamano,
  deshabilitado = false,
  cargando = false,
  icono,
  precio,
  children,
  className,
  onClick,
  type = "button",
  ref,
  ...resto
}: PropiedadesDeBoton) {
  const inactivo = deshabilitado || cargando
  const sobreTinta = (variante ?? "primario") === "primario" && !inactivo

  function alTocar(evento: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) {
    if (inactivo) {
      evento.preventDefault()
      return
    }
    onClick?.(evento)
  }

  const derecha = cargando ? (
    <PuntoTrabajando />
  ) : precio ? (
    <span className={cn("text-meta font-medium tabular-nums", sobreTinta ? "text-luz" : "text-apagado")}>{precio}</span>
  ) : (
    icono
  )

  return (
    <button
      {...resto}
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      aria-disabled={inactivo || undefined}
      aria-busy={cargando || undefined}
      onClick={alTocar}
      className={cn(clasesDeBoton({ variante, tamano }), className)}
    >
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">{children}</span>
      {/* A space, so a price joins the accessible name as "Preparar mi voz US$ 0,33"; a flex row does not draw it. */}{" "}
      {derecha}
    </button>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/confirmacion-en-linea.tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react"
import { Boton } from "./boton"

interface PropiedadesDeConfirmacion {
  /** «gasto» and «neutro» confirm with the primary; «destructivo» with the raised destructive confirm and a secondary way back. */
  trato: "gasto" | "destructivo" | "neutro"
  pregunta: string
  confirmar: string
  volver: string
  alConfirmar: () => void
  alVolver: () => void
  cargando?: boolean
}

/** The confirmation appears in line where the action was; its question takes focus so it is heard. */
export function ConfirmacionEnLinea({
  trato,
  pregunta,
  confirmar,
  volver,
  alConfirmar,
  alVolver,
  cargando = false,
}: PropiedadesDeConfirmacion) {
  const preguntaRef = useRef<HTMLParagraphElement>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    preguntaRef.current?.focus()
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className="confirmacion grid gap-3">
      <p ref={preguntaRef} tabIndex={-1} className="text-cuerpo outline-none">
        {pregunta}
      </p>
      {trato === "destructivo" ? (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5">
          <Boton variante="destructiva" tamano="chico" cargando={cargando} onClick={alConfirmar}>
            {confirmar}
          </Boton>
          <Boton variante="secundario" tamano="chico" onClick={alVolver}>
            {volver}
          </Boton>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Boton cargando={cargando} onClick={alConfirmar}>
            {confirmar}
          </Boton>
          <Boton variante="callado" onClick={alVolver}>
            {volver}
          </Boton>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/volver.tsx
import { CaretLeft } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import Link from "next/link"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

/** Decision 4: the way back is touchable, so it is raised; a back button that is not a link uses the same classes. */
export const CLASES_DE_VOLVER =
  "inline-flex min-h-objetivo w-fit items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control bg-piedra pr-[18px] pl-3 text-cuerpo font-semibold text-tinta shadow-relieve transition-shadow duration-[var(--dur-corta)] ease-salida active:shadow-hundido"

export function Volver({ href, children }: { href: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <Link href={href} className={CLASES_DE_VOLVER}>
      <CaretLeft aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.medio} />
      {children}
    </Link>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the component**

```tsx
// components/ui/estado.tsx
import { Check, Warning, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

export type TipoDePildora = "ok" | "aviso" | "error" | "neutro"

const ICONOS = { ok: Check, aviso: Warning, error: WarningCircle } as const

/**
 * Decision 4: a state is a flat pill, icon and meta 600, never a shadow (The Relief Means Touch Rule). Success and
 * warning are no colours: ink on honda with their icon (The No Yellow Rule). An error is magenta on honda.
 */
export function PildoraDeEstado({
  tipo,
  children,
  className,
}: {
  tipo: TipoDePildora
  children: ReactNode
  className?: string
}) {
  const Icono = tipo === "neutro" ? null : ICONOS[tipo]
  return (
    <span
      data-contorno
      className={cn(
        "pildora inline-flex min-h-pildora max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-control py-1 pr-3.5 pl-2.5 text-meta font-semibold",
        tipo === "neutro" ? "bg-piedra-alta pl-3.5" : "bg-piedra-honda",
        tipo === "error" ? "text-error" : "text-tinta",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {Icono ? <Icono aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} weight="bold" className="shrink-0" /> : null}
      <span>{children}</span>
    </span>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the component**

```tsx
// components/ui/bloque.tsx
import { CheckCircle, Info, Warning, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

export type TipoDeBloque = "aviso" | "error" | "neutro" | "ok"

const ICONOS = { aviso: Warning, error: WarningCircle, neutro: Info, ok: CheckCircle } as const

interface PropiedadesDeBloque {
  tipo: TipoDeBloque
  titulo?: ReactNode
  /** The detail, in ink. */
  children?: ReactNode
  /** A failure with an action: 24 px corners, 18 px padding, the title at body size, the piece title and one action. */
  grande?: boolean
  pieza?: string
  accion?: ReactNode
  /** The error was already announced by another alert of the screen: it is read, not announced again. */
  anunciada?: boolean
  /** An error title that is a message rather than a cause stays in ink. */
  tituloEnTinta?: boolean
  id?: string
  className?: string
}

/** Decision 4: a flat honda block with its icon, never a shadow. Magenta only for an error title, after the icon. */
export function Bloque({
  tipo,
  titulo,
  children,
  grande = false,
  pieza,
  accion,
  anunciada = false,
  tituloEnTinta = false,
  id,
  className,
}: PropiedadesDeBloque) {
  const Icono = ICONOS[tipo]
  const enMagenta = tipo === "error" && !tituloEnTinta
  const clases = cn(
    "grid grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-2.5 bg-piedra-honda text-tinta",
    grande ? "rounded-panel p-[18px] text-cuerpo" : "rounded-bloque px-[14px] py-3 text-meta",
    className,
  )
  const contenido = (
    <>
      <Icono
        aria-hidden="true"
        size={grande ? ICONO.medio : ICONO.chico}
        className={cn("mt-0.5", enMagenta ? "text-error" : "text-tinta")}
      />
      <div className="grid gap-1">
        {titulo ? <p className={cn("font-semibold", enMagenta ? "text-error" : "text-tinta")}>{titulo}</p> : null}
        {pieza ? <p className="font-semibold">{pieza}</p> : null}
        {children ? <div className={grande ? "text-meta" : undefined}>{children}</div> : null}
        {accion ? <div className="mt-2">{accion}</div> : null}
      </div>
    </>
  )
  // <output> is the polite status; only an error interrupts, once.
  if (tipo === "error" && !anunciada)
    return (
      <div role="alert" id={id} data-contorno className={clases}>
        {contenido}
      </div>
    )
  if (tipo === "error")
    return (
      <div id={id} data-contorno className={clases}>
        {contenido}
      </div>
    )
  return (
    <output id={id} data-contorno className={clases}>
      {contenido}
    </output>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/panel.tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** Decision 4: what she only reads sits flat on piedra-alta. Never a panel inside a panel. */
export function Panel({ children, className, id }: { children: ReactNode; className?: string; id?: string }) {
  return (
    <div id={id} data-contorno className={cn("rounded-panel bg-piedra-alta p-5", className)}>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/grupo.tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

interface PropiedadesDeGrupo {
  children: ReactNode
  /** Rows that open nothing: flat on piedra-alta instead of raised. */
  plano?: boolean
  /** The rows are list items. */
  lista?: boolean
  className?: string
}

/** Decision 4: rows that open something live in a raised group, divided by 1 px hairlines; the group has no rule. */
export function Grupo({ children, plano = false, lista = false, className }: PropiedadesDeGrupo) {
  const clases = cn(
    "divide-y divide-hilo rounded-panel px-[18px]",
    plano ? "bg-piedra-alta" : "bg-piedra shadow-relieve",
    className,
  )
  return lista ? (
    <ul data-contorno={plano || undefined} className={clases}>
      {children}
    </ul>
  ) : (
    <div data-contorno={plano || undefined} className={clases}>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}
```

### Task 11 (Lote 2): The disclosure ("Más opciones")

Decision 4, rows "Disclosure" and "Danger disclosure": a native `<details>` (it keeps its content mounted, so a draft
survives closing it, decision 9), open by default when it holds something missing. Four forms: `panel` (raised row
that opens, gates' "Más opciones"), `fila` (a row inside a `Grupo`, Inicio's "Tu semana"), `boton` (the summary is a
full-width small secondary, "Editar el guion") and `linea` (a neutral row with a hairline above, the danger and key
disclosures of Cuentas).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the component**

```tsx
// components/ui/mas-opciones.tsx
"use client"

import { CaretRight } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { type ReactNode, type Ref, useState } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { clasesDeBoton } from "./clases-de-boton"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

type Forma = "panel" | "fila" | "boton" | "linea"

interface PropiedadesDeMasOpciones {
  titulo: string
  /** One meta line under the title ("Avatar: Estudio"). */
  resumen?: ReactNode
  /** Open when it first renders: it holds something missing (decision 9). */
  abiertaAlInicio?: boolean
  forma?: Forma
  /** The boton form's icon, on the right of the label. */
  icono?: ReactNode
  className?: string
  ref?: Ref<HTMLDetailsElement>
  children: ReactNode
}

const SUMARIO = "cursor-pointer list-none [&::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden"

/**
 * Decision 4: a native disclosure. Its content stays mounted while folded, so a draft survives closing it. The open
 * state follows the element (`onToggle`), so React never fights a tap or a screen that opens it through the ref.
 */
export function MasOpciones({
  titulo,
  resumen,
  abiertaAlInicio = false,
  forma = "panel",
  icono,
  className,
  ref,
  children,
}: PropiedadesDeMasOpciones) {
  const [abierta, setAbierta] = useState(abiertaAlInicio)
  return (
    <details
      ref={ref}
      open={abierta}
      onToggle={(evento) => setAbierta(evento.currentTarget.open)}
      className={cn(
        "group",
        forma === "panel" && "rounded-panel bg-piedra px-[18px] shadow-relieve",
        forma === "linea" && "border-t border-hilo",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {forma === "boton" ? (
        <summary
          className={cn(
            SUMARIO,
            clasesDeBoton({ variante: "secundario", tamano: "chico" }),
            "w-full justify-between group-open:shadow-hundido",
          )}
        >
          {titulo}
          {icono}
        </summary>
      ) : (
        <summary
          className={cn(
            SUMARIO,
            "flex items-center gap-3",
            forma === "linea" ? "min-h-objetivo text-meta" : "min-h-fila-baja py-3 text-cuerpo",
            forma === "panel" && "rounded-panel active:shadow-hundido",
          )}
        >
          <span className="grid min-w-0 flex-1">
            <span className="font-semibold text-tinta">{titulo}</span>
            {resumen ? <span className="text-meta font-medium text-apagado">{resumen}</span> : null}
          </span>
          <CaretRight
            aria-hidden="true"
            size={ICONO.medio}
            className="shrink-0 text-apagado transition-transform duration-[var(--dur-media)] ease-salida group-open:rotate-90"
          />
        </summary>
      )}
      <div className={cn("grid gap-3", forma === "panel" ? "border-t border-hilo pt-[14px] pb-[18px]" : "pt-3 pb-1")}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </details>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite the component**

```tsx
// components/ui/linea-de-estado.tsx
import { CalendarBlank, Info } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Bloque } from "./bloque"
import { PildoraDeEstado } from "./estado"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

export type TipoDeEstado = "exito" | "aviso" | "error" | "neutro" | "fecha"

interface PropiedadesDeLinea {
  tipo: TipoDeEstado
  titulo: ReactNode
  /** A label read before the title, in the same weight. */
  rotulo?: string
  /** An error title that is a message rather than a cause stays in ink. */
  tituloEnTinta?: boolean
  detalle?: ReactNode
  accion?: ReactNode
  /** The error was already announced by another alert of the screen: this one is read, not announced again. */
  anunciada?: boolean
  className?: string
}

/**
 * Decision 4: the Calma suiza API, drawn with the Soft UI parts. `exito` is an `ok` pill, `aviso` and `error` are
 * honda blocks (only the error interrupts), `neutro` and `fecha` a flat line with its icon.
 */
export function LineaDeEstado({
  tipo,
  titulo,
  rotulo,
  tituloEnTinta = false,
  detalle,
  accion,
  anunciada = false,
  className,
}: PropiedadesDeLinea) {
  const encabezado = rotulo ? (
    <>
      {rotulo} {titulo}
    </>
  ) : (
    titulo
  )
  if (tipo === "error") {
    return (
      <Bloque
        tipo="error"
        titulo={encabezado}
        tituloEnTinta={tituloEnTinta}
        anunciada={anunciada}
        accion={accion}
        className={className}
      >
        {detalle}
      </Bloque>
    )
  }
  if (tipo === "aviso") {
    // A warning that is only a message reads in the block's body; one with a detail takes the title's weight.
    return detalle === undefined && rotulo === undefined ? (
      <Bloque tipo="aviso" accion={accion} className={className}>
        {titulo}
      </Bloque>
    ) : (
      <Bloque tipo="aviso" titulo={encabezado} accion={accion} className={className}>
        {detalle}
      </Bloque>
    )
  }
  if (tipo === "exito") {
    return (
      <output className={cn("grid justify-items-start gap-1", className)}>
        <PildoraDeEstado tipo="ok">{encabezado}</PildoraDeEstado>
        {detalle ? <span className="text-meta text-tinta">{detalle}</span> : null}
        {accion}
      </output>
    )
  }
  const Icono = tipo === "fecha" ? CalendarBlank : Info
  const clases = cn("flex items-start gap-2.5 text-meta text-tinta", className)
  const contenido = (
    <>
      <Icono aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
      <span className="grid gap-1">
        <span>{encabezado}</span>
        {detalle ? <span>{detalle}</span> : null}
        {accion}
      </span>
    </>
  )
  // A date the screen states is not news: no live region at all.
  if (tipo === "fecha") return <div className={clases}>{contenido}</div>
  return <output className={clases}>{contenido}</output>
}
```

```tsx
// tests/unit/ui-marca-revision.test.tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react"

import { MarcaRevision } from "@/components/ui/marca-revision"
import type { Hallazgo } from "@/lib/dominio/revision"

const CON_FRASE: Hallazgo = {
  regla: "cifra_sin_verificar",
  tipo: "advierte",
  mensaje: "El informe dice «muchas», no «la mayoría».",
  frase: "la mayoría de estas apps",
}
const SIN_FRASE: Hallazgo = { regla: "tema_de_riesgo", tipo: "advierte", mensaje: "El tema es sexualidad." }
const BLOQUEA: Hallazgo = { regla: "cierre", tipo: "bloquea", mensaje: "Falta el cierre." }

describe("MarcaRevision", () => {
  it("asks her to look at the marked phrase and tick 'Lo revisé y está bien'", () => {
    const alCambiar = vi.fn()

    expect(screen.getByText("Revisa la frase marcada")).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText("El informe dice «muchas», no «la mayoría».")).toBeInTheDocument()
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Lo revisé y está bien" }))
    expect(alCambiar).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true)
  })

  it("quotes the phrase only when the script above could not mark it", () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<MarcaRevision hallazgo={CON_FRASE} confirmada={false} alCambiar={vi.fn()} fraseResaltada />)
    expect(screen.queryByText("la mayoría de estas apps")).toBeNull()
    rerender(<MarcaRevision hallazgo={CON_FRASE} confirmada={false} alCambiar={vi.fn()} />)
    expect(screen.getByText("la mayoría de estas apps").tagName.toLowerCase()).toBe("q")

    expect(screen.getByText("Frase revisada")).toBeInTheDocument()

  it("is a flat panel with a 1 px campo border and no side rule, with the id the marked phrase links to", () => {

    const panel = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Revisa la frase marcada" })
    expect(panel).toHaveAttribute("id", "revision-0")
    expect(panel).toHaveClass("bg-piedra-alta", "border", "border-campo", "rounded-panel")
    expect(panel.className).not.toMatch(/shadow-|border-l-/)
  })

  it("has no tick on a blocking finding: an error block that says it has to be fixed", () => {
    render(<MarcaRevision hallazgo={BLOQUEA} confirmada={false} alCambiar={vi.fn()} />)
    expect(screen.queryByRole("checkbox")).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("Hay que corregirlo")
    expect(screen.getByText("Hay que corregirlo")).toHaveClass("text-error")
    expect(screen.getByText("Sin esto no puedes firmar.")).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Editar el guion" })).toBeNull()
  })

  it("offers to open the editor on a blocking finding at gate 1", () => {
    const alEditar = vi.fn()
    render(<MarcaRevision hallazgo={BLOQUEA} confirmada={false} alCambiar={vi.fn()} alEditar={alEditar} />)
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Editar el guion" }))
    expect(alEditar).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })

  it("does not interrupt again for a blocking finding the screen already announced", () => {
    render(<MarcaRevision hallazgo={BLOQUEA} confirmada={false} alCambiar={vi.fn()} anunciada />)
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite the component**

```tsx
// components/ui/marca-revision.tsx
"use client"

import { CheckCircle, Flag } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useId } from "react"
import type { Hallazgo } from "@/lib/dominio/revision"
import { Bloque } from "./bloque"
import { Boton } from "./boton"
import { Casilla } from "./casilla"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

interface PropiedadesDeMarca {
  hallazgo: Hallazgo
  confirmada: boolean
  alCambiar: (confirmada: boolean) => void
  /** A blocking finding listed under an error that already interrupted: it does not interrupt again. */
  anunciada?: boolean
  /** The panel's id: the marked phrase in the script links here. */
  id?: string
  /** The script above marks the phrase: the panel does not quote it again. */
  fraseResaltada?: boolean
  /** Gate 1: a blocking finding offers to open the editor. */
  alEditar?: () => void
}

function tituloDe(hallazgo: Hallazgo, confirmada: boolean): string {
  if (confirmada) return hallazgo.frase ? "Frase revisada" : "Revisado"
  return hallazgo.frase ? "Revisa la frase marcada" : "Revisa este punto"
}

/**
 * Decision 9: a warning is a flat review panel with today's checkbox; a blocking finding is an error block that has to
 * be fixed, not confirmed. No side rule (answered question 1): a flat background with a 1 px campo border.
 */
export function MarcaRevision({
  hallazgo,
  confirmada,
  alCambiar,
  anunciada = false,
  id,
  fraseResaltada = false,
  alEditar,
}: PropiedadesDeMarca) {
  const idTitulo = useId()
  const cita = hallazgo.frase && !fraseResaltada ? <q className="block text-cuerpo">{hallazgo.frase}</q> : null
  if (hallazgo.tipo === "bloquea") {
    return (
      <Bloque
        tipo="error"
        id={id}
        titulo="Hay que corregirlo"
        anunciada={anunciada}
        accion={
          alEditar ? (
            <Boton variante="callado" onClick={alEditar}>
              Editar el guion
            </Boton>
          ) : undefined
        }
      >
        <span className="grid gap-1">
          <span>{hallazgo.mensaje}</span>
          {cita}
          <span>Sin esto no puedes firmar.</span>
        </span>
      </Bloque>
    )
  }
  const Icono = confirmada ? CheckCircle : Flag
  return (
    <section
      id={id}
      aria-labelledby={idTitulo}
      data-contorno
      className="grid scroll-mt-4 gap-2 rounded-panel border border-campo bg-piedra-alta px-[18px] pt-[18px] pb-1.5"
    >
      <p id={idTitulo} className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-cuerpo font-semibold">
        <Icono aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.medio} className="shrink-0" />
        {tituloDe(hallazgo, confirmada)}
      </p>
      <p className="text-meta">{hallazgo.mensaje}</p>
      {cita}
      <Casilla etiqueta="Lo revisé y está bien" marcada={confirmada} alCambiar={alCambiar} />
    </section>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/seccion.tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react"

interface PropiedadesDeSeccion {
  titulo: string
  id: string
  /** The figure on the right: «2», «123 palabras · ≈ 52 s», «1 frase marcada». Text or a node. */
  cifra?: ReactNode
  children: ReactNode
}

/** v6 `.sech` (decision 4): no rule; the label meta 600 in ink on the left, the figure meta 500 apagado on the right. */
export function Seccion({ titulo, id, cifra, children }: PropiedadesDeSeccion) {
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={id} className="mt-8 grid gap-3 first:mt-0">
      <div className="mx-0.5 flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-x-3">
        <h2 id={id} className="text-meta font-semibold text-tinta">
          {titulo}
        </h2>
        {cifra !== undefined ? <span className="text-meta font-medium text-apagado tabular-nums">{cifra}</span> : null}
      </div>
      {children}
    </section>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/punto-trabajando.tsx
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** The dot that pulses while something real is happening (v6 `.punto`). Still under reduced motion. */
export function PuntoTrabajando({ chico = false }: { chico?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <span
      aria-hidden="true"
      data-chico={chico || undefined}
      className={cn("punto-trabajando inline-block shrink-0 rounded-full bg-current", chico ? "size-punto-chico" : "size-punto")}
    />
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/fila-pieza.tsx
import { CaretRight, Warning } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import Link from "next/link"
import { NOMBRE_DE_PILAR, type Pilar } from "@/lib/dominio/pilares"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { ICONO } from "./ui/icono"
import { PuntoTrabajando } from "./ui/punto-trabajando"

interface PropiedadesDeFila {
  titulo: string
  /** What is happening to the piece, in one line: «Guion · 52 s», «Escribiendo el guion · te aviso». */
  estado?: string
  /** A failure's cause, in a honda pill with the warning icon, in place of the state (Inicio, decision 8). */
  causa?: string
  pilar: Pilar | null
  /** Without it the row is not a link (something is happening to the piece and there is nothing to do yet). */
  href?: string
  trabajando?: boolean
  /** An unread notice points at this piece: an ink dot in the left margin. */
  sinLeer?: boolean
  /** The real frame of the video, only when there is one. */
  miniatura?: string
}

/** Decision 4, v6 `.fila`: a row of a group. Pillar, title and state; a chevron when it opens something. */
export function FilaPieza({
  titulo,
  estado,
  causa,
  pilar,
  href,
  trabajando = false,
  sinLeer = false,
  miniatura,
}: PropiedadesDeFila) {
  const contenido = (
    <>
      <span className="grid min-w-0 gap-0.5">
        {pilar ? <span className="text-meta text-apagado">{NOMBRE_DE_PILAR[pilar]}</span> : null}
        <span className="text-cuerpo leading-[1.3] font-semibold">{titulo}</span>
        {causa ? (
          <span className="mt-1.5 inline-flex w-fit items-start gap-1.5 rounded-bloque bg-piedra-honda px-2.5 py-1 text-meta font-semibold">
            <Warning aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            <span className="min-w-0">{causa}</span>
          </span>
        ) : estado ? (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-meta text-tinta">
            {trabajando ? <PuntoTrabajando chico /> : null}
            {estado}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </span>
      {miniatura ? (
        // biome-ignore lint/performance/noImgElement: a signed storage URL of a small frame; next/image adds nothing here.
        <img src={miniatura} alt="" className="h-16 w-12 rounded-bloque object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <span />
      )}
      {href ? <CaretRight aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.medio} className="text-apagado" /> : <span />}
      {sinLeer ? (
        <>
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="absolute top-1/2 -left-3 size-2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-tinta" />
          <span className="sr-only">Sin leer</span>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
  const clases =
    "relative grid min-h-fila grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto_auto] items-center gap-x-3 py-[14px] text-left"

  return (
    <li data-pilar={pilar ?? undefined} className="list-none">
      {href ? (
        <Link
          href={href}
          className={cn(
            clases,
            "transition-colors duration-[var(--dur-corta)] active:bg-piedra-honda motion-reduce:transition-none",
          )}
        >
          {contenido}
        </Link>
      ) : (
        <div className={clases}>{contenido}</div>
      )}
    </li>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/chip.tsx
"use client"

import { Check } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { ICONO } from "./ui/icono"

interface PropiedadesDeChip extends Omit<ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, "aria-pressed"> {
  presionado: boolean
  /** The check of a pressed chip; off where seven day chips share one row. */
  conCheck?: boolean
}

/** Decision 4: a raised 2.75rem pill that reads as a filter; pressed fills with ink and shows a check. */
export function Chip({ presionado, conCheck = true, className, type = "button", children, ...resto }: PropiedadesDeChip) {
  return (
    <button
      {...resto}
      type={type}
      aria-pressed={presionado}
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex min-h-objetivo shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-control bg-piedra px-4 text-meta font-semibold text-tinta shadow-relieve",
        "transition-[box-shadow,background-color,color] duration-[var(--dur-corta)] ease-salida active:shadow-hundido",
        "aria-pressed:bg-tinta aria-pressed:text-luz",
        "aria-disabled:bg-piedra-honda aria-disabled:text-apagado aria-disabled:shadow-none",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {presionado && conCheck ? <Check aria-hidden="true" weight="bold" size={ICONO.chico} /> : null}
      {children}
    </button>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/pestanas.tsx
import Link from "next/link"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

interface OpcionDePestana<T extends string> {
  valor: T
  etiqueta: string
  href: string
}

interface PropiedadesDePestanas<T extends string> {
  etiqueta: string
  actual: T
  opciones: readonly OpcionDePestana<T>[]
}

/**
 * Decision 4, the segmented control: views as links (each has an address and works without a script) on a sunken
 * track; the current one is raised.
 */
export function Pestanas<T extends string>({ etiqueta, actual, opciones }: PropiedadesDePestanas<T>) {
  return (
    <nav aria-label={etiqueta}>
      <ul className="flex gap-1 rounded-control bg-piedra-honda p-1 shadow-hundido">
        {opciones.map((opcion) => {
          const esActual = opcion.valor === actual
          return (
            <li key={opcion.valor} className="flex-1">
              <Link
                href={opcion.href}
                replace
                aria-current={esActual ? "page" : undefined}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-h-objetivo items-center justify-center rounded-control px-3 text-cuerpo transition-shadow duration-[var(--dur-corta)] ease-salida",
                  esActual ? "bg-piedra font-semibold text-tinta shadow-relieve" : "font-medium text-apagado",
                )}
              >
                {opcion.etiqueta}
              </Link>
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  )
}
```

### Task 16 (Lote 2): Sunken boxes, switches and radio rows

Decision 4, rows "Checkbox" (1.65rem box, radius 9 px, honda sunken, 2 px `campo` ring; checked: tinta fill, luz check,
`--hundido-marcado`; the whole 3.25rem row is the target), "Radio / choice" (`FilaConCheck` becomes `Eleccion`: a
1.65rem round sunken radio with a .6rem luz dot when chosen; once one is chosen the others turn apagado 500; the ring
of focus goes around the row) and "Switch" (3.25 x 2rem, track honda sunken with a `campo` ring, thumb piedra raised;
on: track tinta, thumb luz). Rows lose their own borders: their group divides them.

**Files:**
- Modify: `components/ui/casilla.tsx`, `components/ui/interruptor.tsx`, `components/ui/filas.tsx`,
  `components/casillas-codigo.tsx`, `app/globals.css`
- Modify (rename `FilaConCheck` to `Eleccion`): `app/(app)/video/[id]/pantalla-video.tsx`,
  `app/(app)/ideas/idea-propia.tsx`
- Modify: `tests/unit/casilla.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/filas.test.tsx`
