```tsx
// components/ui/casilla.tsx
"use client"

import { Check } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useId } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

interface PropiedadesDeCasilla {
  etiqueta: string
  /** A line under the label (the pillar of an idea): it describes the box without becoming part of its name. */
  detalle?: string
  marcada: boolean
  /** At the limit the box reads as disabled but stays focusable, so a screen reader can find it and read why. */
  deshabilitada?: boolean
  alCambiar: (marcada: boolean) => void
}

/** Decision 4: a sunken 1.65rem box with a campo ring inside a 3.25rem row that is all target. */
export function Casilla({ etiqueta, detalle, marcada, deshabilitada = false, alCambiar }: PropiedadesDeCasilla) {
  const idEtiqueta = useId()
  const idDetalle = useId()
  return (
    <label
      className={cn(
        "relative grid min-h-boton cursor-pointer grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)] items-center gap-x-[14px] py-2 text-cuerpo font-semibold",
        deshabilitada && "cursor-not-allowed text-apagado",
      )}
    >
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        checked={marcada}
        aria-labelledby={idEtiqueta}
        aria-describedby={detalle ? idDetalle : undefined}
        aria-disabled={deshabilitada || undefined}
        onChange={() => {
          if (!deshabilitada) alCambiar(!marcada)
        }}
        className="absolute inset-0 size-full cursor-[inherit] opacity-0"
      />
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          "grid size-casilla place-items-center rounded-casilla",
          detalle ? "self-start" : "self-center",
          marcada
            ? "bg-tinta text-luz shadow-hundido-marcado"
            : deshabilitada
              ? "bg-piedra-honda"
              : "bg-piedra-honda shadow-hundido outline-2 outline-campo",
        )}
      >
        {marcada ? <Check weight="bold" size={ICONO.mini} /> : null}
      </span>
      <span className="grid">
        <span id={idEtiqueta}>{etiqueta}</span>
        {detalle ? (
          <span id={idDetalle} className="text-meta font-medium text-apagado">
            {detalle}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </span>
    </label>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/interruptor.tsx
"use client"

import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

interface PropiedadesDeInterruptor extends Omit<ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, "role" | "type"> {
  encendido: boolean
}

/** Decision 4: a 3.25 x 2rem switch. Off: a sunken track with a campo ring and a raised thumb; on: ink and light. */
export function Interruptor({ encendido, className, ...resto }: PropiedadesDeInterruptor) {
  return (
    <button
      {...resto}
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={encendido}
      className={cn(
        "flex h-interruptor-alto w-interruptor-ancho shrink-0 items-center rounded-control px-1",
        encendido ? "bg-tinta" : "bg-piedra-honda shadow-hundido outline-2 -outline-offset-2 outline-campo",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          "size-[calc(2rem-8px)] rounded-full transition-transform duration-[var(--dur-corta)] ease-salida motion-reduce:transition-none",
          encendido ? "translate-x-[1.25rem] bg-luz" : "bg-piedra shadow-relieve-perilla",
        )}
      />
    </button>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/filas.tsx
"use client"

import { CaretRight, CaretUpDown } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { type ReactNode, useId } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"
import { Interruptor } from "./interruptor"

/** Decision 4: a 3.75rem row of a group that opens another level; the group draws the hairline between rows. */
export function FilaNavegacion({ href, children }: { href: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className="flex min-h-fila-baja items-center justify-between gap-3 text-cuerpo font-semibold text-tinta transition-colors duration-[var(--dur-corta)] active:bg-piedra-honda motion-reduce:transition-none"
    >
      {children}
      <CaretRight aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.medio} className="text-apagado" />
    </Link>
  )
}

interface OpcionDeFila<T extends string> {
  valor: T
  etiqueta: string
}

interface PropiedadesDeFilaConValor<T extends string> {
  etiqueta: string
  valor: T
  alCambiar: (valor: T) => void
  /** «select» (default) lists `opciones`; «time» hands over the native time picker. */
  tipo?: "select" | "time"
  opciones?: readonly OpcionDeFila<T>[]
  /** What the row says on the left when it is not the accessible name («A las» for «Hora de publicación»). */
  titulo?: string
  /** A word after the value («hora de Chile»). */
  sufijo?: string
}

/**
 * Label left, current value right. The native control sits invisible over the whole row, so iOS shows its own wheel
 * and the target is the full row.
 */
export function FilaConValor<T extends string>({
  etiqueta,
  valor,
  alCambiar,
  tipo = "select",
  opciones = [],
  titulo,
  sufijo,
}: PropiedadesDeFilaConValor<T>) {
  const visible = tipo === "select" ? (opciones.find((opcion) => opcion.valor === valor)?.etiqueta ?? valor) : valor
  const clasesDelControl = "absolute inset-0 size-full cursor-pointer opacity-0"
  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-fila-baja items-center justify-between gap-4">
      <span className="text-cuerpo font-semibold">{titulo ?? etiqueta}</span>
      <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-cuerpo text-apagado tabular-nums">
        <span className="tabular-nums">{visible}</span>
        {sufijo ? <span>{sufijo}</span> : null}
        <CaretUpDown aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.medio} />
      </span>
      {tipo === "time" ? (
        <input
          type="time"
          aria-label={etiqueta}
          value={valor}
          onChange={(evento) => alCambiar(evento.target.value as T)}
          className={clasesDelControl}
        />
      ) : (
        <select
          aria-label={etiqueta}
          value={valor}
          onChange={(evento) => alCambiar(evento.target.value as T)}
          className={clasesDelControl}
        >
          {opciones.map((opcion) => (
            <option key={opcion.valor} value={opcion.valor}>
              {opcion.etiqueta}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

interface PropiedadesDeFilaConInterruptor {
  etiqueta: string
  detalle?: string
  encendido: boolean
  alCambiar: (encendido: boolean) => void
}

/** A label around the switch, so the whole row is its target and the click reaches it once. */
export function FilaConInterruptor({ etiqueta, detalle, encendido, alCambiar }: PropiedadesDeFilaConInterruptor) {
  const id = useId()
  return (
    // biome-ignore lint/a11y/noLabelWithoutControl: the control is a button with role=switch, which a label can wrap.
    <label className="flex min-h-fila-baja cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 py-2">
      <span className="grid">
        <span id={id} className="text-cuerpo font-semibold">
          {etiqueta}
        </span>
        {detalle ? <span className="text-meta text-apagado">{detalle}</span> : null}
      </span>
      <Interruptor encendido={encendido} aria-labelledby={id} onClick={() => alCambiar(!encendido)} />
    </label>
  )
}

interface PropiedadesDeEleccion {
  nombre: string
  valor: string
  elegido: boolean
  etiqueta: string
  /** A line under the label; it describes the radio. */
  detalle?: string
  /** A photo before the label (the avatar's look). */
  imagen?: ReactNode
  alElegir: (valor: string) => void
}

/**
 * Decision 4, a choice row: a sunken round radio that fills with ink around a light dot. Put the rows in a container
 * with the class `elecciones`, so the rows not chosen turn apagado once one is.
 */
export function Eleccion({ nombre, valor, elegido, etiqueta, detalle, imagen, alElegir }: PropiedadesDeEleccion) {
  const idDetalle = useId()
  return (
    <label className="relative flex min-h-objetivo cursor-pointer items-center gap-[14px] py-3">
      <input
        type="radio"
        name={nombre}
        value={valor}
        checked={elegido}
        aria-describedby={detalle ? idDetalle : undefined}
        onChange={() => alElegir(valor)}
        className="absolute inset-0 size-full cursor-pointer opacity-0"
      />
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          "grid size-casilla shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full",
          elegido ? "bg-tinta shadow-hundido-marcado" : "bg-piedra-honda shadow-hundido outline-2 outline-campo",
        )}
      >
        {elegido ? <span className="size-[0.6rem] rounded-full bg-luz" /> : null}
      </span>
      {imagen}
      <span className="grid min-w-0">
        <span className="eleccion-titulo text-cuerpo font-semibold">{etiqueta}</span>
        {detalle ? (
          <span id={idDetalle} className="text-meta text-apagado">
            {detalle}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </span>
    </label>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/ui/medidor.tsx
import { Warning } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import { tonoDelMedidor } from "@/lib/diseno/medidor"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"
import { LineaDeEstado } from "./linea-de-estado"

interface PropiedadesDelMedidor {
  gastado: number
  tope: number
  /** «Llevas US$ 22,00 este mes»: the figure always goes in words, the bar is never the only signal. */
  texto: string
}

/** Decision 4: a sunken .625rem meter with an ink fill; from 80 percent the figure adds a warning icon, no colour. */
export function Medidor({ gastado, tope, texto }: PropiedadesDelMedidor) {
  const { tono, porcentaje, alTope } = tonoDelMedidor(gastado, tope)
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-2">
      <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-meta font-medium text-tinta tabular-nums">
        {tono === "aviso" ? <Warning aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} /> : null}
        {texto}
      </p>
      {/* biome-ignore lint/a11y/useSemanticElements: a native meter cannot be drawn as a sunken rounded track on every browser. */}
      <div
        role="meter"
        aria-label="Gasto del mes"
        aria-valuemin={0}
        aria-valuemax={tope}
        aria-valuenow={gastado}
        aria-valuetext={texto}
        className="h-riel overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-piedra-honda shadow-hundido"
      >
        <div className="h-full rounded-control bg-tinta" style={{ width: `${porcentaje}%` }} />
      </div>
      {alTope ? <LineaDeEstado tipo="aviso" titulo="Llegaste al tope de gasto de este mes." /> : null}
    </div>
  )
}
```

### Task 18 (Lote 2): The voice player in two rows, with a raised play

Decision 4, row "Play" (3.5rem piedra raised circle, tinta icon: play, pause, replay when finished; sunken when
pressed) and decision 9, step 4 (play and "0:18 / 0:52" in meta 600 tinta on top, the rail below; after the full
listen the `ok` pill "La escuchaste completa"; the play's name becomes "Escuchar de nuevo" once finished; the
pre-listen hint goes). `useReproduccion` learns `terminado`.

```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react"
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest"
import { ReproductorVoz } from "@/components/reproductor-voz"

const SEGUNDOS = 20

function audio(contenedor: HTMLElement): HTMLAudioElement {
  const elemento = contenedor.querySelector("audio")
  if (!elemento) throw new Error("No hay audio")
  Object.defineProperty(elemento, "duration", { value: SEGUNDOS, configurable: true })
  return elemento
}

function reproducir(elemento: HTMLAudioElement, desde: number, hasta: number) {
  for (let t = desde; t <= hasta + 1e-9; t += 0.25) {
    Object.defineProperty(elemento, "currentTime", { value: t, configurable: true })
    fireEvent.timeUpdate(elemento)
  }
}

describe("ReproductorVoz", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
    vi.spyOn(HTMLMediaElement.prototype, "play").mockResolvedValue(undefined)
    vi.spyOn(HTMLMediaElement.prototype, "pause").mockImplementation(() => undefined)
  })
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks()
  })

  it("plays from one raised 3.5rem circle that sinks when pressed", async () => {
    render(<ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-1" alEscuchar={vi.fn()} />)
    const boton = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Escuchar la voz" })
    await act(async () => {
      fireEvent.click(boton)
    })
    expect(HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    expect(boton).toHaveClass("size-play", "bg-piedra", "shadow-relieve", "active:shadow-hundido")
  })

  it("puts the play and the time on one row and the rail under them", () => {
    render(<ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-2" alEscuchar={vi.fn()} />)
    const fila = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Escuchar la voz" }).parentElement
    expect(fila).toContainElement(screen.getByText(/0:00 \/ /))
    expect(fila).not.toContainElement(screen.getByRole("slider", { name: "Avance de la voz" }))
    expect(screen.getByText(/0:00 \/ /)).toHaveClass("text-meta", "font-semibold", "tabular-nums")
  })

  it("reports that she listened once she heard 95 percent, with the ok pill", () => {
    const alEscuchar = vi.fn()
    const { container } = render(
      <ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-3" alEscuchar={alEscuchar} />,
    )
    reproducir(audio(container), 0, SEGUNDOS)
    expect(alEscuchar).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("La escuchaste completa")
  })

  it("does not count skipping to the end", () => {
    const alEscuchar = vi.fn()
    const { container } = render(
      <ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-4" alEscuchar={alEscuchar} />,
    )
    const elemento = audio(container)
    reproducir(elemento, 0, 2)
    reproducir(elemento, 19, SEGUNDOS)
    expect(alEscuchar).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(screen.queryByText("La escuchaste completa")).toBeNull()
  })

  it("remembers she listened when she comes back to the screen", () => {
    const primera = render(<ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-5" alEscuchar={vi.fn()} />)
    reproducir(audio(primera.container), 0, SEGUNDOS)
    primera.unmount()

    const alEscucharOtraVez = vi.fn()
    render(<ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-5" alEscuchar={alEscucharOtraVez} />)
    expect(screen.getByText("La escuchaste completa")).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(alEscucharOtraVez).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })

  it("names the play 'Escuchar de nuevo' once the voice ended", () => {
    const { container } = render(<ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-6" alEscuchar={vi.fn()} />)
    fireEvent.ended(audio(container))
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Escuchar de nuevo" })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it("says when the voice cannot be loaded", () => {
    render(<ReproductorVoz src={null} clave="voz-7" alEscuchar={vi.fn()} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("No pude cargar la voz. Recarga la pantalla.")
  })

  it("moves the audio when she seeks, and a jump to the end does not count as listening", () => {
    const alEscuchar = vi.fn()
    const { container } = render(
      <ReproductorVoz src="https://almacen.test/voz.mp3" clave="voz-8" alEscuchar={alEscuchar} />,
    )
    const elemento = audio(container)
    fireEvent.loadedMetadata(elemento)
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole("slider", { name: "Avance de la voz" }), { target: { value: "19" } })
    expect(elemento.currentTime).toBe(19)
    reproducir(elemento, 19, SEGUNDOS)
    expect(alEscuchar).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })
})
```

In `components/use-reproduccion.ts`:
- add `const [terminado, setTerminado] = useState(false)` after `const [reproduciendo, setReproduciendo] = useState(false)`;
- in `eventos`, replace `onPlay: () => setReproduciendo(true),` with
  `onPlay: () => {\n      setReproduciendo(true)\n      setTerminado(false)\n    },` and `onEnded: () => setReproduciendo(false),`
  with `onEnded: () => {\n      setReproduciendo(false)\n      setTerminado(true)\n    },`;
- return `terminado` too: `return { elemento, eventos, visto, reproduciendo, terminado, tiempo, duracion, conError, alternar, buscar }`.

```tsx
// components/reproductor-voz.tsx
"use client"

import { ArrowCounterClockwise, Pause, Play } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import type { CSSProperties } from "react"
import { ICONO } from "@/components/ui/icono"
import { LineaDeEstado } from "@/components/ui/linea-de-estado"
import { mmss } from "@/lib/formato"
import { useReproduccion } from "./use-reproduccion"

interface PropiedadesDeVoz {
  /** Signed URL of the voice; null when it could not be signed. */
  src: string | null
  /** The stored file, so "listened" is remembered per voice. */
  clave: string
  /** Called once she has listened to 95 percent, and again on every visit once she did. */
  alEscuchar: () => void
}

/**
 * Decision 9, v6 `.voz`: the raised play and the time on top, the rail below; it never collapses. After a full listen,
 * the ok pill "La escuchaste completa"; once the voice ended, the play replays it.
 */
export function ReproductorVoz({ src, clave, alEscuchar }: PropiedadesDeVoz) {
  const { elemento, eventos, visto, reproduciendo, terminado, tiempo, duracion, conError, alternar, buscar } =
    useReproduccion<HTMLAudioElement>({ clave, alTerminar: alEscuchar })
  const falla = src === null || conError
  const avance = duracion > 0 ? Math.min(100, (tiempo / duracion) * 100) : 0
  const nombre = reproduciendo ? "Pausar la voz" : terminado ? "Escuchar de nuevo" : "Escuchar la voz"
  const Icono = reproduciendo ? Pause : terminado ? ArrowCounterClockwise : Play

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-1">
      {src ? <audio ref={elemento} src={src} preload="metadata" {...eventos} /> : null}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-[14px]">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={alternar}
          disabled={falla}
          aria-label={nombre}
          className="grid size-play shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-piedra text-tinta shadow-relieve transition-shadow duration-[var(--dur-corta)] ease-salida active:shadow-hundido disabled:bg-piedra-honda disabled:text-apagado disabled:shadow-none"
        >
          <Icono aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.grande} weight={Icono === ArrowCounterClockwise ? "bold" : "fill"} />
        </button>
        <span className="text-meta font-semibold text-tinta tabular-nums">
          {mmss(tiempo)} / {mmss(duracion)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <input
        type="range"
        aria-label="Avance de la voz"
        aria-valuetext={`${mmss(tiempo)} de ${mmss(duracion)}`}
        min={0}
        max={duracion > 0 ? duracion : 0}
        step={0.1}
        value={Math.min(tiempo, duracion > 0 ? duracion : 0)}
        disabled={falla || duracion <= 0}
        onChange={(evento) => buscar(Number(evento.target.value))}
        className="avance px-2.5"
        style={{ "--p": `${avance}%` } as CSSProperties}
      />
      {falla ? (
        <LineaDeEstado tipo="error" titulo="No pude cargar la voz. Recarga la pantalla." />
      ) : visto ? (
        <LineaDeEstado tipo="exito" titulo="La escuchaste completa" />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helper and the player**

```ts
// lib/diseno/video.ts
import { mmss } from "@/lib/formato"

/** The badge on the video while it plays and until it was watched whole (decision 11): "faltan 0:24". */
export function faltanDelVideo(entrada: {
  duracion: number
  tiempo: number
  reproduciendo: boolean
  visto: boolean
}): string | null {
  const { duracion, tiempo, reproduciendo, visto } = entrada
  if (visto || !reproduciendo || !(duracion > 0)) return null
  return `faltan ${mmss(Math.max(0, duracion - tiempo))}`
}
```

```tsx
// components/reproductor-video.tsx
"use client"

import { Pause, Play } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import type { CSSProperties } from "react"
import { ICONO } from "@/components/ui/icono"
import { LineaDeEstado } from "@/components/ui/linea-de-estado"
import { faltanDelVideo } from "@/lib/diseno/video"
import { mmss } from "@/lib/formato"
import { useReproduccion } from "./use-reproduccion"

interface PropiedadesDeVideo {
  src: string | null
  /** The stored file, so "watched" is remembered per video. */
  clave: string
  alVerCompleto: () => void
  /** Shows where Instagram puts its own text and buttons over the video. */
  zonas: boolean
}

const INSIGNIA = "insignia-video grid min-h-6 place-items-center rounded-control bg-velo-video px-2.5 text-insignia font-semibold"

/**
 * Decision 11: the 9:16 video at 9.5 x 16.9rem, centred and raised (full width at large text), with "Avatar IA" and,
 * while it plays, what is left. Controls in two rows over the veil: the play and the time, then the rail.
 */
export function ReproductorVideo({ src, clave, alVerCompleto, zonas }: PropiedadesDeVideo) {
  const { elemento, eventos, visto, reproduciendo, tiempo, duracion, conError, alternar, buscar } =
    useReproduccion<HTMLVideoElement>({ clave, alTerminar: alVerCompleto })
  const falla = src === null || conError
  const avance = duracion > 0 ? Math.min(100, (tiempo / duracion) * 100) : 0
  const faltan = faltanDelVideo({ duracion, tiempo, reproduciendo, visto })

  return (
    <>
      {/* Tapping anywhere on the frame plays or pauses; the play button is the keyboard and screen reader way in. */}
      {/* biome-ignore lint/a11y/noStaticElementInteractions: the labelled play button inside is the control. */}
      <div
        role="presentation"
        data-testid="reproductor-video"
        onClick={alternar}
        className="video-puerta relative mx-auto h-[16.9rem] w-[9.5rem] max-w-full overflow-hidden rounded-video bg-video text-luz shadow-relieve"
      >
        {src ? (
          <video
            ref={elemento}
            src={src}
            playsInline
            preload="metadata"
            className="size-full object-cover"
            aria-label="Video de tu avatar"
            {...eventos}
          >
            <track kind="captions" />
          </video>
        ) : null}
        <div className="absolute top-2.5 left-2.5 z-10 grid justify-items-start gap-1">
          <span className={INSIGNIA}>Avatar IA</span>
          {faltan ? <span className={INSIGNIA}>{faltan}</span> : null}
        </div>
        {zonas ? (
          <div aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0">
            <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 grid h-[22%] place-items-center border-t-[1.5px] border-dashed border-luz bg-velo-video px-4 text-center text-insignia font-semibold">
              Aquí Instagram pone el texto y los botones
            </div>
            <div className="absolute top-[40%] right-0 bottom-[22%] w-[16%] border-l-[1.5px] border-dashed border-luz bg-velo-video" />
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {/* biome-ignore lint/a11y/noStaticElementInteractions: only stops a tap on the controls from toggling playback. */}
        {/* biome-ignore lint/a11y/useKeyWithClickEvents: not a control; the button and the range inside are. */}
        <div
          onClick={(evento) => evento.stopPropagation()}
          className="absolute inset-x-2.5 bottom-2.5 z-10 grid rounded-[14px] bg-velo-video px-2 pb-1"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={alternar}
              disabled={falla}
              aria-label={reproduciendo ? "Pausar el video" : "Reproducir el video"}
              className="grid size-objetivo shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-luz disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {reproduciendo ? (
                <Pause aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} weight="fill" />
              ) : (
                <Play aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} weight="fill" />
              )}
            </button>
            <span className="text-insignia tabular-nums">
              {mmss(tiempo)} / {mmss(duracion)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <input
            type="range"
            aria-label="Avance del video"
            aria-valuetext={`${mmss(tiempo)} de ${mmss(duracion)}`}
            min={0}
            max={duracion > 0 ? duracion : 0}
            step={0.1}
            value={Math.min(tiempo, duracion > 0 ? duracion : 0)}
            disabled={falla || duracion <= 0}
            onChange={(evento) => buscar(Number(evento.target.value))}
            className="avance sobre-video"
            style={{ "--p": `${avance}%` } as CSSProperties}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
      {falla ? (
        <LineaDeEstado tipo="error" titulo="No pude cargar el video. Recarga la pantalla." className="mt-3" />
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
}
```

### Task 20 (Lote 2): The script in reading mode marks each finding's phrase

Decision 9, step 5: tone marks as honda chips; each finding's `frase`, at its first occurrence in the shown text,
wrapped in `<mark>` (honda, 2 px tinta underline) linked to its review panel, with the flag icon after it. A phrase that
crosses a tone mark is not marked; its panel quotes it (Task 13's `fraseResaltada`). The link is inline text, exempt
from the 44 px target rule: it carries `data-en-linea`, which the accessibility spec already skips.

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/diseno/resaltado.ts`, `tests/unit/resaltado.test.ts`
- Modify: `components/guion-en-lectura.tsx`, `components/ayuda-de-marcas.tsx`, `tests/unit/guion-en-lectura.test.tsx`

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helper and the components**

```ts
// lib/diseno/resaltado.ts
import { partirEnMarcas } from "@/lib/dominio/etiquetas"

export type TrozoDeLectura =
  | { tipo: "texto" | "marca"; valor: string }
  | { tipo: "frase"; valor: string; indice: number }

interface Rango {
  desde: number
  hasta: number
  indice: number
}

/**
 * The script cut for reading (decision 9): tone marks apart, and each finding's phrase marked at its first occurrence.
 * A phrase that is absent, whose first occurrence crosses a tone mark or overlaps an earlier mark stays unmarked; its
 * review panel quotes it instead. `resaltadas` holds the indexes of `frases` that were marked. Joined back, the pieces
 * are the script, character for character.
 */
export function resaltarFrases(
  guion: string,
  frases: readonly (string | null)[],
): { trozos: TrozoDeLectura[]; resaltadas: ReadonlySet<number> } {
  const partes = partirEnMarcas(guion)
  const textos: { desde: number; hasta: number }[] = []
  let posicion = 0
  for (const parte of partes) {
    if (parte.tipo === "texto") textos.push({ desde: posicion, hasta: posicion + parte.valor.length })
    posicion += parte.valor.length
  }

  const rangos: Rango[] = []
  for (const [indice, frase] of frases.entries()) {
    if (!frase) continue
    const desde = guion.indexOf(frase)
    if (desde < 0) continue
    const hasta = desde + frase.length
    const enUnTexto = textos.some((texto) => texto.desde <= desde && hasta <= texto.hasta)
    const pisaOtra = rangos.some((rango) => desde < rango.hasta && rango.desde < hasta)
    if (enUnTexto && !pisaOtra) rangos.push({ desde, hasta, indice })
  }
  rangos.sort((a, b) => a.desde - b.desde)

  const trozos: TrozoDeLectura[] = []
  posicion = 0
  for (const parte of partes) {
    const inicio = posicion
    const fin = posicion + parte.valor.length
    posicion = fin
    if (parte.tipo === "marca") {
      trozos.push(parte)
      continue
    }
    let cursor = inicio
    for (const rango of rangos) {
      if (rango.desde < inicio || rango.hasta > fin) continue
      if (rango.desde > cursor) trozos.push({ tipo: "texto", valor: guion.slice(cursor, rango.desde) })
      trozos.push({ tipo: "frase", valor: guion.slice(rango.desde, rango.hasta), indice: rango.indice })
      cursor = rango.hasta
    }
    if (cursor < fin) trozos.push({ tipo: "texto", valor: guion.slice(cursor, fin) })
  }
  return { trozos, resaltadas: new Set(rangos.map((rango) => rango.indice)) }
}
```

```tsx
// components/guion-en-lectura.tsx
import { Flag } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import { resaltarFrases } from "@/lib/diseno/resaltado"
import { ICONO } from "./ui/icono"

interface PropiedadesDeLectura {
  guion: string
  /** Each finding's phrase, by the finding's index; null when it quotes none. */
  frases?: readonly (string | null)[]
  /** The id of a finding's review panel, for the link on its marked phrase. */
  idDeRevision?: (indice: number) => string
}

/**
 * Decision 9: the script in reading mode. Tone marks are honda chips; a finding's phrase is marked at its first
 * occurrence and links to its review panel, with the flag after it. Inline text: exempt from the 44 px target rule.
 */
export function GuionEnLectura({ guion, frases = [], idDeRevision }: PropiedadesDeLectura) {
  const { trozos } = resaltarFrases(guion, frases)
  let posicion = 0
  return (
    <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-guion">
      {trozos.map((trozo) => {
        const clave = posicion
        posicion += trozo.valor.length
        if (trozo.tipo === "marca")
          return (
            <span
              key={clave}
              className="mr-0.5 inline-block rounded-control bg-piedra-honda px-2.5 align-[2px] text-meta font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-tinta"
            >
              {trozo.valor}
            </span>
          )
        if (trozo.tipo === "frase")
          return (
            <span key={clave}>
              <mark
                data-en-linea
                className="rounded-[4px] bg-piedra-honda px-0.5 text-tinta underline decoration-tinta decoration-2 underline-offset-3 [box-decoration-break:clone]"
              >
                {idDeRevision ? (
                  <a href={`#${idDeRevision(trozo.indice)}`} className="text-inherit">
                    {trozo.valor}
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  trozo.valor
                )}
              </mark>
              <Flag aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} className="ml-1 inline align-[-0.1em]" />
            </span>
          )
        return <span key={clave}>{trozo.valor}</span>
      })}
    </p>
  )
}
```

### Task 21 (Lote 2): The sign bar sticks to the bottom as the screen's last element

Decision 6: bars are `position: sticky; bottom: 0`, the last child of the screen (no more `--barra-max` padding; `main`
drops its end padding when a bar is present). Decision 4, row "Sign track" and decision 9, step 7: the bar holds,
top to bottom, what is above the track (an action error, a blocking block or the "Falta:" line), the track and the
consequence line (centred meta apagado, `aria-describedby` of the track). Code fact: `data-barra` is already used by
`components/barra-de-metrica.tsx` (the metric bar), so the sign bar is marked `data-barra-de-firma` instead of the
spec's `data-barra`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the component and its CSS**

```tsx
// components/ui/barra-de-firma.tsx
import { Check, Info } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { ICONO } from "./icono"

interface PropiedadesDeBarra {
  /** Above the track: an action error, a blocking block or the "Falta:" line. */
  arriba?: ReactNode
  /** Under the track, centred: what the signature does. Its id describes the track. */
  consecuencia?: string
  idConsecuencia?: string
  /** The track. */
  children: ReactNode
}

/**
 * Decisions 5 and 6: the one high bar of a signing screen. Sticky at the bottom, as the screen's last element: it sits
 * in the flow at the end of the page, so the page keeps no padding for it. Static at large text (Task 45).
 */
export function BarraDeFirma({ arriba, consecuencia, idConsecuencia, children }: PropiedadesDeBarra) {
  return (
    <section
      aria-label="Firma"
      data-barra-de-firma
      className="barra-de-firma sticky bottom-0 z-10 -mx-5 mt-8 grid gap-3 rounded-t-barra bg-piedra px-5 pt-[14px] pb-[calc(14px+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] shadow-relieve-alto-fijo"
    >
      {arriba}
      {children}
      {consecuencia ? (
        <p id={idConsecuencia} className="text-center text-meta text-apagado">
          {consecuencia}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  )
}

/** "Falta: guardar tus cambios · escuchar tu voz": what still closes the signature, after the info icon. */
export function LineaDeFalta({ id, cosas }: { id: string; cosas: readonly string[] }) {
  return (
    <p id={id} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-meta font-medium text-tinta">
      <Info aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
      <span>
        <b className="font-semibold">Falta:</b> {cosas.join(" · ")}
      </span>
    </p>
  )
}

/** Gate 2 when nothing is missing: "Video visto" and "Texto", each with its check. */
export function ResumenListo({ id, cosas }: { id: string; cosas: readonly string[] }) {
  return (
    <p id={id} className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-meta font-semibold text-tinta">
      {cosas.map((cosa) => (
        <span key={cosa} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Check aria-hidden="true" weight="bold" size={ICONO.chico} />
          {cosa}
        </span>
      ))}
    </p>
  )
}
```

```css
}
```

### Task 22 (Lote 2): One sign control: drag, or tap to arm and tap again to sign

Decision 5 (the whole section). The "Aprobar" button goes. The track is the slider (`role="slider"`, `tabIndex 0`):
a drag of the thumb signs on release at 96 % or more and springs back otherwise; a pointer that moves more than 8 px
is a drag (from the thumb) or a scroll (anywhere else), never a tap; a tap (down and up within 8 px, on the track or the
thumb) arms: the thumb travels to the end, the trail fills with `--firma-rastro`, the label and `aria-valuetext` read
"Toca otra vez para firmar"; a second tap between 400 ms and 4 s signs, under 400 ms is ignored, after 4 s it
disarms. Enter, Space and End sign; ArrowUp and ArrowRight arm, then sign on the second press; ArrowDown, ArrowLeft,
Home and Escape disarm. While the action travels the thumb stays at the end, the trail is solid and the label reads
"Firmado"; a refused signature returns it to rest. The label is "Desliza para firmar" on both gates. Hidden hint:
"Enter o Espacio firman. También puedes tocar dos veces: la primera prepara la firma."

```ts
describe("tap model", () => {
  it("counts a pointer that moved 8 px or less as a tap", () => {
    expect(TOQUE_MAXIMO_PX).toBe(8)
    expect(esToque({ dx: 8, dy: 0 })).toBe(true)
    expect(esToque({ dx: 8.1, dy: 0 })).toBe(false)
    expect(esToque({ dx: 6, dy: 6 })).toBe(false)
  })

  it("arms at rest, ignores a second tap under 400 ms, signs from 400 ms to 4 s, and arms again after", () => {
    expect([ESPERA_MINIMA_MS, ARMADO_MAXIMO_MS]).toEqual([400, 4000])
    expect(respuestaAlToque(null, 1000)).toBe("armar")
    expect(respuestaAlToque(1000, 1399)).toBe("ignorar")
    expect(respuestaAlToque(1000, 1400)).toBe("firmar")
    expect(respuestaAlToque(1000, 5000)).toBe("firmar")
    expect(respuestaAlToque(1000, 5001)).toBe("armar")
  })
})
```

```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react"
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest"
import { DeslizarParaAprobar } from "@/components/deslizar-para-aprobar"

const pista = () => screen.getByRole("slider", { name: "Desliza para firmar" })
const pulgar = () => screen.getByTestId("pulgar-firma")

function caja(ancho: number): DOMRect {
  return { width: ancho, left: 0, right: ancho, top: 0, bottom: 56, height: 56, x: 0, y: 0, toJSON: () => ({}) }
}

beforeEach(() => {
  // Track 356 px, thumb 48 px, 4 px inset on each side: free travel 300 px.
  vi.spyOn(HTMLElement.prototype, "getBoundingClientRect").mockImplementation(function (this: HTMLElement) {
    return caja(this.dataset.testid === "pulgar-firma" ? 48 : 356)
  })
  HTMLElement.prototype.setPointerCapture = vi.fn()
  HTMLElement.prototype.releasePointerCapture = vi.fn()
  vi.useFakeTimers()
})

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers()
  vi.restoreAllMocks()
})

function arrastrar(hasta: number) {
  fireEvent.pointerDown(pulgar(), { pointerId: 1, clientX: 0, clientY: 20 })
  fireEvent.pointerMove(pista(), { pointerId: 1, clientX: hasta, clientY: 20 })
  fireEvent.pointerUp(pista(), { pointerId: 1, clientX: hasta, clientY: 20 })
}

function tocar() {
  fireEvent.pointerDown(pista(), { pointerId: 1, clientX: 200, clientY: 20 })
  fireEvent.pointerUp(pista(), { pointerId: 1, clientX: 202, clientY: 21 })
}

describe("DeslizarParaAprobar: drag", () => {
  it("starts unsigned, labelled 'Desliza para firmar'", () => {
    render(<DeslizarParaAprobar habilitado alFirmar={vi.fn()} />)
    expect(pista()).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuenow", "0")
    expect(pista()).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuetext", "Sin firmar")
    expect(pista()).toHaveTextContent("Desliza para firmar")
  })

  it("signs on release after a drag of the thumb past 96 percent, once", () => {
    const alFirmar = vi.fn()
    render(<DeslizarParaAprobar habilitado alFirmar={alFirmar} />)
    arrastrar(300)
    arrastrar(300)
    expect(alFirmar).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    expect(pista()).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuetext", "Firmado")

  it("springs back when released before 96 percent", () => {
    const alFirmar = vi.fn()

    fireEvent.pointerUp(pista(), { pointerId: 1, clientX: 100, clientY: 60 })
    expect(alFirmar).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(pista()).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuetext", "Sin firmar")
  })
})

describe("DeslizarParaAprobar: tap to arm, tap to sign (WCAG 2.5.7)", () => {
  it("arms on a tap: the thumb goes to the end and the label says to tap again", () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the model**

Append to `lib/diseno/deslizador.ts`:

```ts
/** Decision 5: one label on both gates, the armed and the sent states, and how to sign without dragging. */
export const ETIQUETA_DE_FIRMA = "Desliza para firmar"
export const TEXTO_ARMADO = "Toca otra vez para firmar"
export const TEXTO_FIRMADO = "Firmado"
/** What the slider's value reads at rest (v6 `aria-valuetext`). */
export const TEXTO_SIN_FIRMAR = "Sin firmar"
export const PISTA_DE_FIRMA = "Enter o Espacio firman. También puedes tocar dos veces: la primera prepara la firma."

/** A pointer that moved more than this is a drag or a scroll, never a tap. */
export const TOQUE_MAXIMO_PX = 8
/** A second tap sooner than this is an accidental double tap, or VoiceOver's double tap delivered as two events. */
export const ESPERA_MINIMA_MS = 400
/** An armed control waits this long for the second tap, then goes back to rest. */
export const ARMADO_MAXIMO_MS = 4000

export function esToque({ dx, dy }: { dx: number; dy: number }): boolean {
  return Math.hypot(dx, dy) <= TOQUE_MAXIMO_PX
}

export type RespuestaAlToque = "armar" | "firmar" | "ignorar"

/** What a tap (or an arming key) does, given when the control was armed; null means at rest. */
export function respuestaAlToque(armadoEn: number | null, ahora: number): RespuestaAlToque {
  if (armadoEn === null) return "armar"
  const pasado = ahora - armadoEn
  if (pasado < ESPERA_MINIMA_MS) return "ignorar"
  return pasado <= ARMADO_MAXIMO_MS ? "firmar" : "armar"
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the control**

```tsx
// components/deslizar-para-aprobar.tsx
"use client"

import { ArrowRight, Check } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { type KeyboardEvent, type PointerEvent, useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { ICONO } from "@/components/ui/icono"
import {
  ARMADO_MAXIMO_MS,
  apruebaAlSoltar,
  ETIQUETA_DE_FIRMA,
  esToque,
  PISTA_DE_FIRMA,
  progresoDeArrastre,
  respuestaAlToque,
  TEXTO_ARMADO,
  TEXTO_FIRMADO,
  TEXTO_SIN_FIRMAR,
  UMBRAL_DE_APROBACION,
} from "@/lib/diseno/deslizador"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const MARGEN = 4
const TECLAS_QUE_FIRMAN = new Set(["Enter", " ", "End"])
const TECLAS_QUE_ARMAN = new Set(["ArrowUp", "ArrowRight"])
const TECLAS_QUE_DESARMAN = new Set(["ArrowDown", "ArrowLeft", "Home", "Escape"])

interface PropiedadesDeDeslizar {
  /** False until the gate's conditions are met; the track stays focusable so a screen reader can read why. */
  habilitado: boolean
  /** True while the signature travels to the server: the thumb stays at the end. */
  enviando?: boolean
  /** Ids of what describes the track (the "Falta:" line, the consequence), space separated. */
  describidoPor?: string
  alFirmar: () => void
}

interface Gesto {
  x: number
  y: number
  /** It started on the thumb at rest: a move past 8 px is a drag. Anywhere else it is a scroll. */
  enPulgar: boolean
  recorrido: number
  arrastre: boolean
}

/**
 * Decision 5: the one sign control, on both gates. Drag the thumb past 96 percent and release; or tap anywhere on the
 * track to arm it and tap again between 400 ms and 4 s to sign (WCAG 2.5.7: single pointer, no path; 2.5.2: it signs
 * on the up event). Enter, Space and End sign; ArrowUp and ArrowRight arm, then sign; ArrowDown, ArrowLeft, Home and
 * Escape disarm.
 */
export function DeslizarParaAprobar({ habilitado, enviando = false, describidoPor, alFirmar }: PropiedadesDeDeslizar) {
  const [valor, setValor] = useState(0)
  const [armado, setArmado] = useState(false)
  const [firmado, setFirmado] = useState(false)
  const [arrastrando, setArrastrando] = useState(false)
  // Guards against a second signature before React re-renders with `firmado`.
  const yaFirmo = useRef(false)
  const armadoEn = useRef<number | null>(null)
  const gesto = useRef<Gesto | null>(null)
  const pista = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const pulgar = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null)
  const idPista = useId()
  const activo = habilitado && !enviando

  // The signature did not go through (or finished): the thumb goes back so she can try again.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enviando) {
      yaFirmo.current = false
      setFirmado(false)
      setValor((actual) => (actual >= UMBRAL_DE_APROBACION ? 0 : actual))
    }
  }, [enviando])

  // A control that closes while armed (she started editing, a refresh brought a block) disarms.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (habilitado) return
    armadoEn.current = null
    setArmado(false)
    if (!yaFirmo.current) setValor(0)
  }, [habilitado])

  // Armed for 4 s at most; then the thumb goes back.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!armado) return
    const temporizador = setTimeout(() => {
      armadoEn.current = null
      setArmado(false)
      setValor(0)
    }, ARMADO_MAXIMO_MS)
    return () => clearTimeout(temporizador)
  }, [armado])

  function desarmar() {
    armadoEn.current = null
    setArmado(false)
    setValor(0)
  }

  function armar() {
    armadoEn.current = Date.now()
    setArmado(true)
    setValor(100)
  }

  function firmar() {
    if (yaFirmo.current || !activo) return
    yaFirmo.current = true
    armadoEn.current = null
    setArmado(false)
    setFirmado(true)
    setValor(100)
    alFirmar()
  }

  function tocar() {
    if (!activo || yaFirmo.current) return
    const respuesta = respuestaAlToque(armadoEn.current, Date.now())
    if (respuesta === "armar") armar()
    else if (respuesta === "firmar") firmar()
  }

  function alBajar(evento: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (!activo || yaFirmo.current || !pista.current) return
    const sobrePulgar = evento.target instanceof Node && (pulgar.current?.contains(evento.target) ?? false)
    const anchoDePista = pista.current.getBoundingClientRect().width
    const anchoDePulgar = pulgar.current?.getBoundingClientRect().width ?? 0
    gesto.current = {
      x: evento.clientX,
      y: evento.clientY,
      enPulgar: sobrePulgar && !armado,
      recorrido: anchoDePista - anchoDePulgar - 2 * MARGEN,
      arrastre: false,
    }
    if (sobrePulgar && !armado) evento.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(evento.pointerId)
  }

  function alMover(evento: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    const actual = gesto.current
    if (!actual) return
    if (!actual.arrastre) {
      if (esToque({ dx: evento.clientX - actual.x, dy: evento.clientY - actual.y })) return
      // Past 8 px: a drag when it started on the thumb, otherwise a scroll that signs nothing.
      if (!actual.enPulgar) {
        gesto.current = null
        return
      }
      actual.arrastre = true
      setArrastrando(true)
    }
    setValor(progresoDeArrastre({ inicioX: actual.x, actualX: evento.clientX, recorrido: actual.recorrido }))
  }

  function alSoltar(evento: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    const actual = gesto.current
    gesto.current = null
    if (!actual) return
    if (!actual.arrastre) {
      if (esToque({ dx: evento.clientX - actual.x, dy: evento.clientY - actual.y })) tocar()
      return
    }
    setArrastrando(false)
    const final = progresoDeArrastre({ inicioX: actual.x, actualX: evento.clientX, recorrido: actual.recorrido })
    if (apruebaAlSoltar(final)) firmar()
    else setValor(0)
  }

  function alCancelar() {
    const actual = gesto.current
    gesto.current = null
    if (actual?.arrastre) {
      setArrastrando(false)
      setValor(0)
    }
  }

  function alTeclear(evento: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (yaFirmo.current) return
    if (TECLAS_QUE_FIRMAN.has(evento.key)) {
      evento.preventDefault()
      firmar()
    } else if (TECLAS_QUE_ARMAN.has(evento.key)) {
      evento.preventDefault()
      tocar()
    } else if (TECLAS_QUE_DESARMAN.has(evento.key)) {
      evento.preventDefault()
      if (armado) desarmar()
    }
  }

  const texto = firmado ? TEXTO_FIRMADO : armado ? TEXTO_ARMADO : ETIQUETA_DE_FIRMA
  const posicion = `calc(${MARGEN}px + (100% - var(--pulgar-firma) - ${2 * MARGEN}px) * ${valor / 100})`

  return (
    <div
      ref={pista}
      role="slider"
      tabIndex={0}
      aria-label={ETIQUETA_DE_FIRMA}
      aria-valuemin={0}
      aria-valuemax={100}
      aria-valuenow={Math.round(valor)}
      aria-valuetext={firmado ? TEXTO_FIRMADO : armado ? TEXTO_ARMADO : TEXTO_SIN_FIRMAR}
      aria-disabled={!activo}
      aria-describedby={describidoPor ? `${describidoPor} ${idPista}` : idPista}
      data-testid="pista-de-firma"
      onPointerDown={alBajar}
      onPointerMove={alMover}
      onPointerUp={alSoltar}
      onPointerCancel={alCancelar}
      onKeyDown={alTeclear}
      className={cn(
        "pista-de-firma relative flex min-h-[var(--pista-firma)] touch-pan-y items-center rounded-control bg-piedra-honda shadow-hundido select-none focus-visible:outline-3 focus-visible:outline-offset-3",
        activo ? "cursor-grab" : "cursor-not-allowed",
      )}
    >
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          "absolute inset-y-0 left-0 rounded-control",
          firmado ? "bg-firma" : "bg-firma-rastro",
          !arrastrando && "transition-[width] duration-[var(--dur-larga)] ease-vuelta",
        )}
        style={{ width: valor === 0 ? 0 : `calc(${posicion} + var(--pulgar-firma) / 2)` }}
      />
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          "relative z-10 flex-1 py-2 pr-4 pl-[calc(var(--pulgar-firma)+var(--hueco-etiqueta))] text-center text-cuerpo leading-tight font-semibold text-balance",
          firmado ? "text-luz" : activo ? "text-tinta" : "text-apagado",
        )}
      >
        {texto}
      </span>
      <span
        ref={pulgar}
        aria-hidden="true"
        data-testid="pulgar-firma"
        className={cn(
          "absolute top-1/2 z-20 grid size-[var(--pulgar-firma)] -translate-y-1/2 touch-none place-items-center rounded-full",
          !arrastrando && "transition-[left] duration-[var(--dur-larga)] ease-vuelta",
          firmado
            ? "bg-luz text-firma"
            : activo
              ? "bg-firma text-luz shadow-relieve-pulgar"
              : "bg-piedra text-apagado outline-2 -outline-offset-2 outline-campo",
        )}
        style={{ left: posicion }}
      >
        {firmado ? (
          <Check aria-hidden="true" weight="bold" size={ICONO.grande} />
        ) : (
          <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.grande} />
        )}
      </span>
      <span id={idPista} className="sr-only">
        {PISTA_DE_FIRMA}
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
              <p id={idMotivo} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-meta font-medium text-tinta">
                <Info aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                {motivo}
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </>
        }
      >
        <DeslizarParaAprobar
          habilitado={motivo === undefined}
          enviando={aprobacion.pendiente}
          describidoPor={motivo ? `${idMotivo} ${idConsecuencia}` : idConsecuencia}
          alFirmar={() =>
            aprobacion.ejecutar(
              () => aprobarGuionAccion(datos.id, version, avatarElegido),
              // The undo on the video screen sends the version she signed at, never one a refresh brought later.
              (firmada) => router.replace(`/guion/${datos.id}?firmada=${firmada.version}`),
            )
          }
        />
      </BarraDeFirma>
```

- [ ] **Step 9: End to end: drag, two taps, keyboard**

In `tests/e2e/ayudas.ts`, change `import type { BrowserContext, Page } from "@playwright/test"` to
`import { type BrowserContext, expect, type Page } from "@playwright/test"`, add
`import { ETIQUETA_DE_FIRMA, TEXTO_ARMADO } from "@/lib/diseno/deslizador"`, and append:

```ts
/** Drags the thumb of the sign track to the end, the way a finger does. */
export async function deslizarHastaElFinal(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const pista = page.getByRole("slider", { name: ETIQUETA_DE_FIRMA })
  await pista.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded()
  const caja = await pista.boundingBox()
  const pulgar = await page.getByTestId("pulgar-firma").boundingBox()
  if (!caja || !pulgar) throw new Error("La firma no tiene caja")
  const y = pulgar.y + pulgar.height / 2
  await page.mouse.move(pulgar.x + pulgar.width / 2, y)
  await page.mouse.down()
  await page.mouse.move(caja.x + caja.width, y, { steps: 14 })
  await page.mouse.up()
}

/** Signs without dragging: a tap arms the track, a second one after 400 ms signs (decision 5). */
export async function firmarConDosToques(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const pista = page.getByRole("slider", { name: ETIQUETA_DE_FIRMA })
  await pista.click()
  await expect(pista).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuetext", TEXTO_ARMADO)
  // A second tap sooner than 400 ms is ignored as an accidental double tap: wait like a person would.
  await page.waitForTimeout(500)
  await pista.click()
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the component and its placement**

```tsx
// components/ui/aviso.tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { createPortal } from "react-dom"

export const DURACION_DEL_AVISO_MS = 8000

interface PropiedadesDeAviso {
  mensaje: string
  alCerrar: () => void
  alDeshacer?: () => void
  /**
   * Given when the caller tracks its undo: the toast then stays, with "Deshaciendo…", until the caller closes it on
   * the result. Without it the toast closes as soon as "Deshacer" is tapped.
   */
  deshaciendo?: boolean
  /** Focus "Deshacer" on mount, for a toast whose undo is what she most likely wants next. */
  enfocarDeshacer?: boolean
  duracionMs?: number
  /** "nav": above the bottom nav. "arriba": at the top, on a screen with a sign bar (answered question 2). */
  lugar?: "nav" | "arriba"
}

/**
 * Decisions 4 and 6: the toast, in ink with high relief, gone after 8 s, with "Deshacer" only when the action can be
 * undone. Portalled to <body>, so a screen's layout never moves it; globals.css places it by `data-lugar`.
 */
export function Aviso({
  mensaje,
  alCerrar,
  alDeshacer,
  deshaciendo,
  enfocarDeshacer = false,
  duracionMs = DURACION_DEL_AVISO_MS,
  lugar = "nav",
}: PropiedadesDeAviso) {
  // A live region is announced when its content changes, not when it arrives already filled: it mounts empty in
  // <body> on one commit and gets its message on the next.
  const [montado, setMontado] = useState(false)
  const [lleno, setLleno] = useState(false)
  const botonDeshacer = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const sigueDeshaciendo = deshaciendo === true

  useEffect(() => {
    setMontado(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!montado) return
    setLleno(true)
    if (enfocarDeshacer) botonDeshacer.current?.focus()
  }, [montado, enfocarDeshacer])

  useEffect(() => {
    // An undo on its way decides how the toast ends; the timer must not close it first.
    if (sigueDeshaciendo) return
    const temporizador = setTimeout(alCerrar, duracionMs)
    return () => clearTimeout(temporizador)
  }, [alCerrar, duracionMs, sigueDeshaciendo])

  if (!montado) return null
  return createPortal(
    <output
      data-lugar={lugar}
      className="aviso flex items-center gap-3 rounded-toast bg-tinta py-2.5 pr-2.5 pl-5 text-cuerpo font-semibold text-luz shadow-relieve-alto"
    >
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">{lleno ? mensaje : null}</span>
      {alDeshacer ? (
        <button
          ref={botonDeshacer}
          type="button"
          // aria-disabled, not disabled: the button keeps her focus while the undo is on its way.
          aria-disabled={sigueDeshaciendo || undefined}
          className="min-h-objetivo shrink-0 rounded-control bg-luz px-[18px] text-cuerpo font-semibold text-tinta focus-visible:outline-luz aria-disabled:opacity-70"
          onClick={() => {
            if (sigueDeshaciendo) return
            alDeshacer()
            if (deshaciendo === undefined) alCerrar()
          }}
        >
          {sigueDeshaciendo ? "Deshaciendo…" : "Deshacer"}
        </button>
      ) : null}
    </output>,
    document.body,
  )
}
```

In `app/globals.css`, replace the block

```css
/* The toast slides in 8 px and fades; a transition (not a keyframe) so a second toast retargets smoothly. */
.aviso {
  transition:
    transform var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida),
    opacity var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida);
}
```

with

```css
/* Decision 6: the toast sits above the bottom nav, at the top on a signing screen (never over the sign bar), and at
   the bottom at large text, where nothing is pinned. It slides in 8 px and fades; a transition (not a keyframe) so a
   second toast retargets smoothly. */
.aviso {
  position: fixed;
  left: 16px;
  right: 16px;
  z-index: var(--capa-toast);
  margin-inline: auto;
  max-width: 520px;
  transition:
    transform var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida),
    opacity var(--dur-media) var(--curva-salida);
}
.aviso[data-lugar="nav"] {
  bottom: calc(var(--alto-nav) + 12px);
}
.aviso[data-lugar="arriba"] {
  top: calc(env(safe-area-inset-top) + 12px);
}
/* From md the nav moves to the top: the toast sits at the bottom. */
@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .aviso[data-lugar="nav"] {
    bottom: calc(16px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  }
}
```

`<Aviso mensaje={aviso} alCerrar={cerrarAviso} sobreBarra />` with `<Aviso mensaje={aviso} alCerrar={cerrarAviso} lugar="arriba" />`.

```tsx
// components/nav-inferior.tsx
"use client"

import { ChartBar, GearSix, House } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation"
import { ICONO } from "@/components/ui/icono"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const PESTANAS = [
  { href: "/", etiqueta: "Inicio", icono: House },
  { href: "/metricas", etiqueta: "Métricas", icono: ChartBar },
  { href: "/ajustes", etiqueta: "Ajustes", icono: GearSix },
] as const

// Ideas and Calendario hang from Inicio: they have no tab of their own, so Inicio stays current on them.
const RUTAS_QUE_CUELGAN_DE_INICIO = ["/ideas", "/calendario"]

function estaActiva(ruta: string, pestana: (typeof PESTANAS)[number]): boolean {
  if (pestana.href === "/") {
    return ruta === "/" || RUTAS_QUE_CUELGAN_DE_INICIO.some((raiz) => ruta === raiz || ruta.startsWith(`${raiz}/`))
  }
  return ruta === pestana.href || ruta.startsWith(`${pestana.href}/`)
}

// On the screens that sign something, the sign bar takes the bottom of the screen.
const RUTAS_DE_DETALLE = ["/guion/", "/video/"]

/** Decision 4: a raised bar on piedra; the current tab is a sunken honda pill, "where you are", not an action. */
export function NavInferior() {
  const ruta = usePathname()
  if (RUTAS_DE_DETALLE.some((prefijo) => ruta.startsWith(prefijo))) return null

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Secciones"
      className="nav-inferior sticky bottom-0 z-10 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 rounded-t-panel bg-piedra px-[14px] pt-2 pb-[calc(24px+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] shadow-relieve-fijo md:top-0 md:bottom-auto md:rounded-t-none md:rounded-b-panel md:pb-2"
    >
      {PESTANAS.map((pestana) => {
        const activa = estaActiva(ruta, pestana)
        const Icono = pestana.icono
        return (
          <Link
            key={pestana.href}
            href={pestana.href}
            aria-current={activa ? "page" : undefined}
            className={cn(
              "grid min-h-nav content-center justify-items-center gap-0.5 rounded-control py-2 text-meta transition-shadow duration-[var(--dur-corta)] ease-salida",
              activa ? "bg-piedra-honda font-semibold text-tinta shadow-hundido" : "font-medium text-apagado",
            )}
          >
            <Icono aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.grande} />
            <span className="nav-etiqueta">{pestana.etiqueta}</span>
          </Link>
        )
      })}
    </nav>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helpers**

```ts
// lib/diseno/revision.ts
import type { Hallazgo } from "@/lib/dominio/revision"

const cuenta = (cantidad: number, uno: string, varios: string) => (cantidad === 1 ? `1 ${uno}` : `${cantidad} ${varios}`)

/** Every pending finding is a warning that quotes a phrase: the screen talks about phrases. */
function todasConFrase(pendientes: readonly Hallazgo[]): boolean {
  return pendientes.every((hallazgo) => hallazgo.tipo === "advierte" && Boolean(hallazgo.frase))
}

/**
 * The figure beside "2. Revisa el guion" (decision 9, step 5): phrases when what is pending are marked phrases, "N por
 * revisar" otherwise, "Revisado" once nothing is pending, nothing without findings.
 */
export function cifraDeRevision(pendientes: readonly Hallazgo[], total: number): string | null {
  if (total === 0) return null
  if (pendientes.length === 0) return "Revisado"
  if (todasConFrase(pendientes)) return pendientes.length === 1 ? "1 frase marcada" : `${pendientes.length} frases marcadas`
  return `${pendientes.length} por revisar`
}

/** The "Falta:" item of the warnings she has not ticked (decisions 9 and 11): "revisar 1 frase", "revisar 2 avisos". */
export function revisarPendientes(sinRevisar: readonly Hallazgo[]): string | null {
  if (sinRevisar.length === 0) return null
  return todasConFrase(sinRevisar)
    ? `revisar ${cuenta(sinRevisar.length, "frase", "frases")}`
    : `revisar ${cuenta(sinRevisar.length, "aviso", "avisos")}`
}
```

```ts
// app/(app)/guion/[id]/falta-para-firmar.ts
import { TEXTO_SIN_AVATARES } from "@/components/elegir-avatar"
import { revisarPendientes } from "@/lib/diseno/revision"
import type { Hallazgo } from "@/lib/dominio/revision"

export interface EstadoDeLaFirma {
  sinGuardar: boolean
  /** Her voice of this text is being made. */
  preparando: boolean
  /** The take of her voice on the screen; null when there is none. */
  voz: string | null
  /** The take she heard to the end. */
  escuchada: string | null
  bloqueos: number
  /** The warnings she has not ticked yet. */
  sinRevisar: readonly Hallazgo[]
  sinAvatares: boolean
  avatarElegido: boolean
  /** HeyGen's balance or the month's cap cannot cover the video (today's text). */
  bloqueoVideo: string | null
}

export type FaltaParaFirmar = { tipo: "nada" } | { tipo: "bloqueo"; texto: string } | { tipo: "falta"; cosas: string[] }

/**
 * What keeps gate 1's signature closed (decision 9, step 7). A balance, the cap or no looks at all is a block, said
 * whole; otherwise every missing item, in the order she would do them, for the "Falta:" line.
 */
export function faltaParaFirmar(estado: EstadoDeLaFirma): FaltaParaFirmar {
  if (estado.bloqueoVideo !== null) return { tipo: "bloqueo", texto: estado.bloqueoVideo }
  if (estado.sinAvatares) return { tipo: "bloqueo", texto: TEXTO_SIN_AVATARES }
  const voz = estado.preparando
    ? "que termine tu voz"
    : estado.voz === null
      ? "preparar tu voz"
      : estado.escuchada !== estado.voz
        ? "escuchar tu voz"
        : null
  const cosas = [
    estado.sinGuardar ? "guardar tus cambios" : null,
    voz,
    estado.bloqueos > 0 ? "corregir el guion" : null,
    revisarPendientes(estado.sinRevisar),
    estado.avatarElegido ? null : "elegir el avatar",
  ].filter((cosa): cosa is string => cosa !== null)
  return cosas.length === 0 ? { tipo: "nada" } : { tipo: "falta", cosas }
}
```

```ts
/** The consequence under gate 1's track (design 2026-10-16, decision 9): what the signature does, its cost, the undo. */
export function lineaDeFirma(video: { segundos: number; usd: number } | null): string {
  const ventana = `${VENTANA_DESHACER_MS / 1000} s para deshacer`
  return video === null ? `Graba el video · ${ventana}` : `Graba el video · ${dolares(video.usd)} · ${ventana}`
}
```

```ts
// Soft UI copy (design 2026-10-16, decisions 9, 10 and 13, and its copy table).
export const TEXTO_PEDIR_CAMBIOS = "Escribo un guion nuevo y este se descarta."
export const TEXTO_SIN_OBSERVACIONES = "Claude revisó el guion y no encontró observaciones. Tú decides."
export const TEXTO_NO_PUDE_PREPARAR_VOZ = "No pude preparar tu voz."
export const TEXTO_DESHAZLO = "Si te arrepientes, deshazlo ahora. No se cobra nada."
export const TEXTO_GRABANDO = "Estoy grabando tu video."
export const TEXTO_GRABANDO_DETALLE = "Unos 5 min. Te aviso cuando esté listo."
export const TEXTO_NO_TERMINE = "No pude terminar el video."
export const TEXTO_NO_ESCRIBI = "No pude escribir el guion"
export const TEXTO_VIDEO_DETENIDO = "El video está detenido"

/** "Grabo tu video en 8 s": the seconds really left of the undo window, computed once (decision 10). */
export const textoGraboEn = (quedanMs: number) =>
  `Grabo tu video en ${Math.min(VENTANA_DESHACER_MS / 1000, Math.ceil(quedanMs / 1000))} s`

/** A voice that could not be made (decision 9, step 4): its title, and the rest of the reason as its detail. */
export function separarFallaDeVoz(explicacion: string): { titulo: string; detalle: string | null } {
  if (!explicacion.startsWith(TEXTO_NO_PUDE_PREPARAR_VOZ)) return { titulo: TEXTO_NO_PUDE_PREPARAR_VOZ, detalle: explicacion }
  const resto = explicacion.slice(TEXTO_NO_PUDE_PREPARAR_VOZ.length).trim()
  return { titulo: TEXTO_NO_PUDE_PREPARAR_VOZ, detalle: resto === "" ? null : resto }
}
```

```tsx
const { pedirVozAccion } = vi.hoisted(() => ({ pedirVozAccion: vi.fn() }))

const BOTONES = {

function grabacion(clave: string | null, sobre: Partial<DatosDeGrabacion> = {}): DatosDeGrabacion {

function escucharEntera(contenedor: HTMLElement) {
  const audio = contenedor.querySelector("audio")
  if (!audio) throw new Error("No hay audio")
  Object.defineProperty(audio, "duration", { value: SEGUNDOS, configurable: true })
  for (let t = 0; t <= SEGUNDOS; t += 0.25) {
    Object.defineProperty(audio, "currentTime", { value: t, configurable: true })
    fireEvent.timeUpdate(audio)
  }
}

})

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks()
})

describe("VozParaFirmar", () => {
  it("is the first step of gate 1", () => {
    render(
      <VozParaFirmar piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={grabacion("voces/p1/t-x-0.mp3")} explicacion={null} alEscuchar={vi.fn()} sinGuardar={false} />,
    )
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "1. Escucha tu voz" })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it("treats another take as unheard: the player starts over and reports the new take once she hears it", () => {
    const alEscuchar = vi.fn()
    const { container, rerender } = render(
      <VozParaFirmar piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={grabacion("voces/p1/t-x-0.mp3")} explicacion={null} alEscuchar={alEscuchar} sinGuardar={false} />,
    )
    escucharEntera(container)
    expect(alEscuchar).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("voces/p1/t-x-0.mp3")

    rerender(
      <VozParaFirmar piezaId="p1" version={6} datos={grabacion("voces/p1/t-x-1.mp3")} explicacion={null} alEscuchar={alEscuchar} sinGuardar={false} />,
    )
    expect(screen.queryByText("La escuchaste completa")).toBeNull()
    escucharEntera(container)
    expect(alEscuchar).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("voces/p1/t-x-1.mp3")
    expect(screen.getByText("La escuchaste completa")).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it("without a voice, offers 'Preparar mi voz' in a neutral block with its cost on the button", async () => {
    render(
      <VozParaFirmar piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={grabacion(null, { botones: BOTONES })} explicacion={null} alEscuchar={vi.fn()} sinGuardar={false} />,
    )
    expect(screen.getByText("Falta preparar tu voz.")).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText(BOTONES.saldoVoz)).toBeInTheDocument()
    const boton = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Preparar mi voz US$ 0,33" })
    fireEvent.click(boton)
    await waitFor(() => expect(pedirVozAccion).toHaveBeenCalledWith("p1", 5, false))
  })

  it("turns a voice that could not be made into an error block: the title, then the rest of the reason", () => {
    render(
      <VozParaFirmar
        piezaId="p1"
        version={5}
        datos={grabacion(null)}
        explicacion="No pude preparar tu voz. Vuelve a intentarlo con Preparar mi voz."
        alEscuchar={vi.fn()}
        sinGuardar={false}
      />,
    )
    const alerta = screen.getByRole("alert")
    expect(screen.getByText("No pude preparar tu voz.")).toHaveClass("text-error")
    expect(alerta).toHaveTextContent("Vuelve a intentarlo con Preparar mi voz.")
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Preparar mi voz" })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it("keeps 'Preparar mi voz' closed while the script has unsaved changes, and says why", () => {
    render(<VozParaFirmar piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={grabacion(null)} explicacion={null} alEscuchar={vi.fn()} sinGuardar />)
    const accion = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Preparar mi voz" })
    expect(accion).toHaveAttribute("aria-disabled", "true")
    expect(accion).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Guarda tus cambios antes de grabar tu voz.")
    fireEvent.click(accion)
    expect(pedirVozAccion).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it("keeps 'Preparar mi voz' closed with the text of what does not reach", () => {
    const datos = grabacion(null, { botones: { ...BOTONES, bloqueoVoz: TOPE } })
    render(<VozParaFirmar piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={datos} explicacion={null} alEscuchar={vi.fn()} sinGuardar={false} />)
    const accion = screen.getByRole("button", { name: /^Preparar mi voz/ })
    expect(accion).toHaveAttribute("aria-disabled", "true")
    expect(accion).toHaveAccessibleDescription(TOPE)
  })

  it("says her voice is being made as a polite working line", () => {
    const datos = grabacion(null, { estado: "grabando_voz" })
    render(<VozParaFirmar piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={datos} explicacion={null} alEscuchar={vi.fn()} sinGuardar={false} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("Estoy preparando tu voz. Tarda unos segundos.")
  })
})

describe("GrabarDeNuevo", () => {
  it("makes a new take of the same text, with its cost on the right", async () => {
    render(<GrabarDeNuevo piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={grabacion("voces/p1/t-x-0.mp3", { botones: BOTONES })} sinGuardar={false} />)
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Grabar la voz de nuevo US$ 0,33" }))
    await waitFor(() => expect(pedirVozAccion).toHaveBeenCalledWith("p1", 5, true))
    expect(screen.getByText(BOTONES.saldoVoz)).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it("stays closed while the script has unsaved changes, and says why", () => {
    render(<GrabarDeNuevo piezaId="p1" version={5} datos={grabacion("voces/p1/t-x-0.mp3")} sinGuardar />)
    const accion = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Grabar la voz de nuevo" })
    expect(accion).toHaveAttribute("aria-disabled", "true")
    expect(accion).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Guarda tus cambios antes de grabar tu voz.")
  })
})
```

```tsx
// app/(app)/guion/[id]/voz-para-firmar.tsx
"use client"

import { Info } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useId } from "react"
import { ReproductorVoz } from "@/components/reproductor-voz"
import { Seccion } from "@/components/seccion"
import { Bloque } from "@/components/ui/bloque"
import { Boton } from "@/components/ui/boton"
import { LineaDeSaldo } from "@/components/ui/costo"
import { ICONO } from "@/components/ui/icono"
import { PuntoTrabajando } from "@/components/ui/punto-trabajando"
import { useAccion } from "@/components/use-accion"
import type { DatosDeGrabacion } from "@/lib/datos/grabacion"
import { pedirVozAccion } from "../../piezas/acciones"
import { separarFallaDeVoz, TEXTO_FALTA_VOZ, TEXTO_GUARDA_ANTES_DE_VOZ, TEXTO_PREPARANDO_VOZ } from "./textos"

interface PropiedadesDeVoz {
  piezaId: string
  /** The version of the screen: a request on a piece that moved on is refused. */
  version: number
  datos: DatosDeGrabacion
  /** Why the last voice could not be made; given only when there is no voice. */
  explicacion: string | null
  /** Which take she heard to the end. */
  alEscuchar: (clave: string) => void
  /** The editor holds changes she has not saved: a voice now would speak the old text and lose her draft. */
  sinGuardar: boolean
}

/** Why the voice buttons are closed, if they are: unsaved changes first, else what does not reach. */
function razonDeVoz(datos: DatosDeGrabacion, sinGuardar: boolean): string | null {
  if (sinGuardar) return TEXTO_GUARDA_ANTES_DE_VOZ
  return datos.botones?.bloqueoVoz ?? null
}

/** Under a voice button: its reason when it is closed, the ElevenLabs balance when it is open. */
function LineaBajoElBoton({ id, razon, datos }: { id: string; razon: string | null; datos: DatosDeGrabacion }) {
  if (razon !== null)
    return (
      <p id={id} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-meta font-medium text-tinta">
        <Info aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
        {razon}
      </p>
    )
  return datos.botones ? <LineaDeSaldo>{datos.botones.saldoVoz}</LineaDeSaldo> : null
}

/**
 * Decision 9, step 4: "1. Escucha tu voz". Her take of this script in the player, the take being made as a working
 * line, or, without a voice, a block with "Preparar mi voz" and its cost (an error block when the last one failed).
 */
export function VozParaFirmar({ piezaId, version, datos, explicacion, alEscuchar, sinGuardar }: PropiedadesDeVoz) {
  const pedir = useAccion()
  const idRazon = useId()
  const { voz, botones } = datos
  const razon = razonDeVoz(datos, sinGuardar)
  const falla = explicacion ? separarFallaDeVoz(explicacion) : null

  return (
    <Seccion titulo="1. Escucha tu voz" id="voz">
      {datos.estado === "grabando_voz" ? (
        <output className="flex items-center gap-3 text-boton font-semibold">
          <PuntoTrabajando />
          {TEXTO_PREPARANDO_VOZ}
        </output>
      ) : voz ? (
        // Keyed by the take: "she heard it" belongs to one file, and a new take starts unheard.
        <ReproductorVoz key={voz.clave} src={voz.url} clave={voz.clave} alEscuchar={() => alEscuchar(voz.clave)} />
      ) : (
        <Bloque
          tipo={falla ? "error" : "neutro"}
          grande
          titulo={falla ? falla.titulo : TEXTO_FALTA_VOZ}
          accion={
            <div className="grid gap-2">
              <Boton
                precio={botones?.montoVoz}
                cargando={pedir.pendiente}
                deshabilitado={razon !== null}
                aria-describedby={razon !== null ? idRazon : undefined}
                onClick={() => pedir.ejecutar(() => pedirVozAccion(piezaId, version, false))}
              >
                Preparar mi voz
              </Boton>
              <LineaBajoElBoton id={idRazon} razon={razon} datos={datos} />
            </div>
          }
        >
          {falla?.detalle}
        </Bloque>
      )}
      {pedir.error ? <Bloque tipo="error" titulo={pedir.error} /> : null}
    </Seccion>
  )
}

/** Inside "Editar el guion" (decision 9, step 5): a new take of the same text, its cost on the right. */
export function GrabarDeNuevo({
  piezaId,
  version,
  datos,
  sinGuardar,
}: Pick<PropiedadesDeVoz, "piezaId" | "version" | "datos" | "sinGuardar">) {
  const pedir = useAccion()
  const idRazon = useId()
  const razon = razonDeVoz(datos, sinGuardar)
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-2">
      <Boton
        variante="secundario"
        tamano="chico"
        className="w-full"
        precio={datos.botones?.montoVoz}
        cargando={pedir.pendiente}
        deshabilitado={razon !== null}
        aria-describedby={razon !== null ? idRazon : undefined}
        onClick={() => pedir.ejecutar(() => pedirVozAccion(piezaId, version, true))}
      >
        Grabar la voz de nuevo
      </Boton>
      <LineaBajoElBoton id={idRazon} razon={razon} datos={datos} />
      {pedir.error ? <Bloque tipo="error" titulo={pedir.error} /> : null}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// components/elegir-avatar.tsx
"use client"

import { useId } from "react"
import { Eleccion } from "@/components/ui/filas"
import { LineaDeEstado } from "@/components/ui/linea-de-estado"

export interface AvatarParaElegir {
  id: string
  nombre: string
  /** A short-lived signed link to the preview photo; null when it could not be signed. */
  imagenUrl: string | null
}

interface PropiedadesDeElegirAvatar {
  avatares: readonly AvatarParaElegir[]
  /** Nothing is pre-selected by WAV: null until she picks, or the look she signed with before an undo. */
  elegido: string | null
  alElegir: (avatarId: string) => void
}

export const TEXTO_SIN_AVATARES = "Todavía no hay avatares para elegir: avísale a Federico."

/** Decision 9, step 6: WAV's looks as choice rows with their round photo. A native radio group: arrows come free. */
export function ElegirAvatar({ avatares, elegido, alElegir }: PropiedadesDeElegirAvatar) {
  const grupo = useId()

  if (avatares.length === 0) return <LineaDeEstado tipo="aviso" titulo={TEXTO_SIN_AVATARES} />

  return (
    <fieldset className="elecciones grid border-0 p-0">
      <legend className="sr-only">Avatar</legend>
      {avatares.map((avatar) => (
        <Eleccion
          key={avatar.id}
          nombre={grupo}
          valor={avatar.id}
          elegido={elegido === avatar.id}
          etiqueta={avatar.nombre}
          alElegir={alElegir}
          imagen={
            avatar.imagenUrl ? (
              // A signed R2 link that expires: next/image would only proxy and cache it. The name labels the row.
              // biome-ignore lint/performance/noImgElement: see above
              <img src={avatar.imagenUrl} alt="" className="size-foto shrink-0 rounded-full bg-piedra-honda object-cover" />
            ) : undefined
          }
        />
      ))}
    </fieldset>
  )
}
```

### Task 29 (Lote 3): Gate 1 in the order of v6

Decision 9, steps 1 to 8, and answered questions 1 and 2. Top to bottom: back "Inicio"; meta row; title; the date line
"Si lo firmas hoy, sale el **...**" (calendar icon, date in tinta 600: the raspberry on the date goes); a phrase HeyGen
refused as a warning block; "1. Escucha tu voz" (Task 27); "2. Revisa el guion" with its figure, the tone-mark help, the
script in a reading panel with each finding's phrase marked (Task 20), one review panel per finding (Task 13), and
"Editar el guion" (a disclosure whose summary is a full-width small secondary; inside today's `EditorDeGuion`, "Grabar
la voz de nuevo", "Pedir cambios" with "Escribo un guion nuevo y este se descarta." and today's confirmation; open by
default when a finding blocks; in `grabando_voz` only the caption read only and the closed "Pedir cambios" with its

```tsx
    act(() => {

    expect(screen.queryByText("Listo, no grabé nada. Puedes seguir revisando.")).toBeNull()
```
