```tsx
// app/(app)/ajustes/cuentas-conectadas.tsx
"use client"

import { ArrowRight, Warning } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { type FormEvent, useCallback, useRef, useState, useTransition } from "react"
import { Aviso } from "@/components/ui/aviso"
import { Boton } from "@/components/ui/boton"
import { clasesDeBoton } from "@/components/ui/clases-de-boton"
import { PildoraDeEstado } from "@/components/ui/estado"
import { Grupo } from "@/components/ui/grupo"
import { ICONO } from "@/components/ui/icono"
import { LineaDeEstado } from "@/components/ui/linea-de-estado"
import { MasOpciones } from "@/components/ui/mas-opciones"
import type { EstadoDeLlave } from "@/lib/acciones/ajustes"
import type { Resultado } from "@/lib/acciones/resultado"
import type { FilaDeCuenta } from "@/lib/ajustes/presentar"

const MENSAJE_DE_LLAVE: Record<EstadoDeLlave, string> = {
  comprobada: "Guardada y comprobada.",
  sin_comprobar: "Guardada. No pude comprobarla ahora, la reviso más tarde.",
  sin_voz: "Guardada. Falta tu voz de ElevenLabs: avísale a Federico.",
}
const TEXTO_DESCONECTAR = "Tus videos dejarán de salir solos ahí y te quedarán para subir a mano."
const MENSAJE_DESCONECTADO =
  "LinkedIn quedó desconectado. Si quieres, también puedes quitar el permiso desde la configuración de tu cuenta de LinkedIn."

interface PropiedadesDeCuentas {
  filas: readonly FilaDeCuenta[]
  guardarLlave: (cuenta: string, llave: string) => Promise<Resultado<{ estado: EstadoDeLlave }>>
  desconectarLinkedin: () => Promise<Resultado>
}

/** Decision 12 (v6 screens 6 and 6b): each account with its state as a pill, what it does, and its actions. */
export function CuentasConectadas({ filas, guardarLlave, desconectarLinkedin }: PropiedadesDeCuentas) {
  const [abierta, setAbierta] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [mensaje, setMensaje] = useState<string | null>(null)
  // Stable, so a re-render while the toast is up does not restart its 8 s.
  const cerrarMensaje = useCallback(() => setMensaje(null), [])

  return (
    <div className="grid">
      <Grupo plano lista>
        {filas.map((fila) => (
          <li key={fila.cuenta} id={`cuenta-${fila.cuenta}`} className="grid scroll-mt-4 gap-2.5 py-[18px]">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <span className="text-[1.25rem] font-semibold">{fila.nombre}</span>
              <PildoraDeEstado tipo={fila.tono}>{fila.estado}</PildoraDeEstado>
            </div>
            {fila.detalle ? <p className="text-meta text-apagado">{fila.detalle}</p> : null}
            {fila.cuenta === "linkedin" ? (
              <AccionesDeLinkedin
                fila={fila}
                desconectar={desconectarLinkedin}
                alDesconectar={() => setMensaje(MENSAJE_DESCONECTADO)}
              />
            ) : fila.cuenta === "instagram" ? (
              // A plain link: the route answers with a redirect to Instagram, which a client navigation cannot follow.
              <a
                href="/api/meta/conectar"
                className={clasesDeBoton({ variante: "secundario", tamano: "chico", className: "w-fit" })}
              >
                {fila.tono === "aviso" && fila.estado === "Falta" ? "Conectar Instagram" : "Renovar Instagram"}
              </a>
            ) : abierta === fila.cuenta ? (
              <FormularioDeLlave
                fila={fila}
                guardarLlave={guardarLlave}
                alGuardar={(estado) => {
                  setAbierta(null)
                  setMensaje(MENSAJE_DE_LLAVE[estado])
                }}
                alCancelar={() => setAbierta(null)}
              />
            ) : fila.estado === "Falta" ? (
              <Boton variante="callado" onClick={() => setAbierta(fila.cuenta)}>
                {`Agregar la llave de ${fila.nombre}`}
              </Boton>
            ) : (
              // WAV's operation, not Coni's (answered question 3): folded, open when the key was refused.
              <MasOpciones forma="linea" titulo="Más opciones" abiertaAlInicio={fila.tono === "error"}>
                <Boton variante="callado" onClick={() => setAbierta(fila.cuenta)}>
                  {`Cambiar la llave de ${fila.nombre}`}
                </Boton>
              </MasOpciones>
            )}
          </li>
        ))}
      </Grupo>
      {mensaje ? <Aviso mensaje={mensaje} alCerrar={cerrarMensaje} /> : null}
    </div>
  )
}

/** LinkedIn: renew (the primary when it needs her), and disconnect behind a danger disclosure. */
function AccionesDeLinkedin({
  fila,
  desconectar,
  alDesconectar,
}: {
  fila: FilaDeCuenta
  desconectar: () => Promise<Resultado>
  alDesconectar: () => void
}) {
  const peligro = useRef<HTMLDetailsElement>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pendiente, empezar] = useTransition()

  if (!fila.conectada)
    return (
      <a
        href="/api/linkedin/conectar"
        className={clasesDeBoton({ variante: "secundario", tamano: "chico", className: "w-fit" })}
      >
        Conectar LinkedIn
      </a>
    )

  const urgente = fila.tono === "aviso" || fila.tono === "error"
  function cerrar() {
    if (peligro.current) peligro.current.open = false
  }

  return (
    <>
      <a
        href="/api/linkedin/conectar"
        className={
          urgente
            ? clasesDeBoton({ variante: "primario" })
            : clasesDeBoton({ variante: "secundario", tamano: "chico", className: "w-fit" })
        }
      >
        Renovar LinkedIn
        {urgente ? <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.grande} /> : null}
      </a>
      <MasOpciones ref={peligro} forma="linea" titulo="Desconectar LinkedIn">
        <div data-contorno className="grid gap-2.5 rounded-bloque bg-piedra-honda p-[14px]">
          <p className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-meta text-tinta">
            <Warning aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.chico} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            {TEXTO_DESCONECTAR}
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5">
            <Boton
              variante="destructiva"
              tamano="chico"
              cargando={pendiente}
              onClick={() =>
                empezar(async () => {
                  const resultado = await desconectar()
                  if (!resultado.ok) {
                    setError(resultado.error)
                    return
                  }
                  cerrar()
                  alDesconectar()
                })
              }
            >
              Sí, desconectar
            </Boton>
            <Boton variante="secundario" tamano="chico" onClick={cerrar}>
              Cancelar
            </Boton>
          </div>
          {error ? <LineaDeEstado tipo="error" titulo={error} /> : null}
        </div>
      </MasOpciones>
    </>
  )
}

function FormularioDeLlave({
  fila,
  guardarLlave,
  alGuardar,
  alCancelar,
}: {
  fila: FilaDeCuenta
  guardarLlave: PropiedadesDeCuentas["guardarLlave"]
  alGuardar: (estado: EstadoDeLlave) => void
  alCancelar: () => void
}) {
  const [llave, setLlave] = useState("")
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pendiente, empezar] = useTransition()
  const id = `llave-${fila.cuenta}`

  function enviar(evento: FormEvent) {
    evento.preventDefault()
    const limpia = llave.trim()
    if (limpia === "") return
    setError(null)
    empezar(async () => {
      const resultado = await guardarLlave(fila.cuenta, limpia)
      if (!resultado.ok) {
        setError(resultado.error)
        return
      }
      alGuardar(resultado.estado)
    })
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={enviar} className="grid gap-3">
      <label className="mx-0.5 text-meta font-semibold" htmlFor={id}>
        {`Llave de ${fila.nombre}`}
      </label>
      <input
        id={id}
        type="password"
        autoComplete="off"
        autoCapitalize="off"
        spellCheck={false}
        value={llave}
        onChange={(evento) => setLlave(evento.target.value)}
        aria-describedby={`${id}-ayuda`}
        className="campo text-cuerpo text-tinta"
      />
      <p id={`${id}-ayuda`} className="mx-0.5 text-meta text-apagado">
        Pégala completa. La reviso con {fila.nombre.split(" ")[0]} y queda guardada cifrada.
      </p>
      {error ? <LineaDeEstado tipo="error" titulo={error} /> : null}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <Boton variante="secundario" onClick={alCancelar}>
          Cancelar
        </Boton>
        <Boton type="submit" cargando={pendiente} deshabilitado={llave.trim() === ""}>
          Guardar y comprobar
        </Boton>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}
```

```tsx
// app/(app)/ajustes/cuentas/page.tsx
import { Suspense } from "react"
import { Seccion } from "@/components/seccion"
import { Bloque } from "@/components/ui/bloque"
import { Volver } from "@/components/ui/volver"
import { avisoDeInstagram } from "@/lib/ajustes/instagram-aviso"
import { avisoDeLinkedin } from "@/lib/ajustes/linkedin-aviso"
import { filaDeCuenta } from "@/lib/ajustes/presentar"
import { exigirConi } from "@/lib/sesion/coni"
import { desconectarLinkedinAccion, guardarLlaveAccion } from "../acciones"
import { BloqueDeEstado } from "../bloque-de-estado"
import { CuentasConectadas } from "../cuentas-conectadas"
import { revisionDeCuentas } from "../revision"
import { SaldosDeLaPagina } from "../saldos"

/** «Cuentas» as its own level of Ajustes (decision 12): each account, its keys and the balances. */
export default async function Cuentas({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ instagram?: string; linkedin?: string }>
}) {
  const { db } = await exigirConi()
  const { instagram, linkedin } = await searchParams
  const { data: ajustes, error } = await db.from("ajustes").select("zona").maybeSingle()
  if (error) throw new Error(`No pude leer los ajustes: ${error.message}`, { cause: error })
  if (!ajustes) throw new Error("Falta la fila de ajustes.")
  const { count: dispositivos, error: errorDeDispositivos } = await db
    .from("suscripciones_push")
    .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })
  if (errorDeDispositivos) throw new Error(`No pude leer tus aparatos: ${errorDeDispositivos.message}`)
  const aviso = avisoDeInstagram(instagram) ?? avisoDeLinkedin(linkedin)

  return (
    <div>
      <Volver href="/ajustes">Ajustes</Volver>
      <h1 className="mt-5 text-pantalla text-balance">Cuentas</h1>
      <p className="mt-1.5 text-meta text-apagado">
        Conectadas a Conidolly: lo que la app necesita para escribir, grabar y publicar.
      </p>

      {aviso ? (
        <Bloque tipo={aviso.tipo} titulo={aviso.titulo} className="mt-6">
          {aviso.texto}
        </Bloque>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-6">
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          <BloqueDeEstado zona={ajustes.zona} avisosActivos={(dispositivos ?? 0) > 0} mostrar="bueno" />
        </Suspense>
      </div>

      <section aria-label="Cuentas" className="mt-8">
        <Suspense fallback={<EsqueletoDeCuentas />}>
          <CuentasDeLaPagina />
        </Suspense>
      </section>

      <Seccion titulo="Saldos" id="saldos">
        <Suspense fallback={<EsqueletoDeCuentas />}>
          <SaldosDeLaPagina zona={ajustes.zona} />
        </Suspense>
      </Seccion>
    </div>
  )
}

async function CuentasDeLaPagina() {
  const ahora = Date.now()
  const filas = (await revisionDeCuentas()).map((estado) => filaDeCuenta(estado, ahora))
  return (
    <CuentasConectadas
      filas={filas}
      guardarLlave={guardarLlaveAccion}
      desconectarLinkedin={desconectarLinkedinAccion}
    />
  )
}

function EsqueletoDeCuentas() {
  return (
    <output className="grid gap-3" aria-label="Revisando las cuentas">
      {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((fila) => (
        <div key={fila} className="esqueleto h-3 w-full" />
      ))}
    </output>
  )
}
```

(`avisoDeInstagram` and `avisoDeLinkedin` answer `{ tipo: "ok" | "aviso" | "error", titulo, texto? }` (`lib/ajustes/instagram-aviso.ts`); the
three types are `Bloque` types, and `LineaDeEstado` with its `TIPO_DE_AVISO` map leaves the page.)

```tsx
// app/(app)/ajustes/bloque-de-estado.tsx
import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle, Info, Warning } from "@phosphor-icons/react/dist/ssr"
import { clasesDeBoton } from "@/components/ui/clases-de-boton"
import { PildoraDeEstado } from "@/components/ui/estado"
import { ICONO } from "@/components/ui/icono"
import { estadoDeAjustes } from "@/lib/ajustes/estado"
import { revisionDeCuentas } from "./revision"

const ICONOS = { configurando: Info, todo_bien: CheckCircle, atencion: Warning, sin_revision: Info } as const

/**
 * The status of the setup as a flat honda block with its steps (decision 12). «pendiente»: the block while something
 * is missing or needs her, on Ajustes; «bueno» is good news and lives with the accounts, so each page asks for its half.
 */
export async function BloqueDeEstado({
  zona,
  avisosActivos,
  mostrar,
}: {
  zona: string
  avisosActivos: boolean
  mostrar: "pendiente" | "bueno"
}) {
  const bloque = estadoDeAjustes({ cuentas: await revisionDeCuentas(), avisosActivos, ahora: Date.now(), zona })
  if ((bloque.tipo === "todo_bien") !== (mostrar === "bueno")) return null
  const Icono = ICONOS[bloque.tipo]

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="estado-titulo"
      data-contorno
      className="grid gap-4 rounded-bloque bg-piedra-honda px-[14px] py-3"
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-2.5 gap-y-1">
        <Icono aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.medio} className="mt-0.5 text-tinta" />
        <h2 id="estado-titulo" className="text-cuerpo font-semibold">
          {bloque.titulo}
        </h2>
        {bloque.texto ? <p className="col-start-2 text-meta">{bloque.texto}</p> : null}
      </div>
      {bloque.tipo === "configurando" ? (
        <ul className="grid divide-y divide-campo/30">
          {bloque.pasos.map((paso) => (
            <li key={paso.etiqueta} className="flex min-h-boton items-center justify-between gap-3 text-meta">
              {paso.etiqueta}
              <PildoraDeEstado tipo={paso.listo ? "ok" : "aviso"} className="bg-piedra">
                {paso.listo ? "Listo" : "Falta"}
              </PildoraDeEstado>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
      {bloque.accion ? (
        <a href={bloque.accion.href} className={clasesDeBoton({ variante: "primario", className: "mt-1" })}>
          {bloque.accion.etiqueta}
          <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" size={ICONO.grande} />
        </a>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  )
}
```

(A pill on the honda block sits on piedra, so the state still reads as a shape; `PildoraDeEstado` merges the
`className` with `cn`.)

## Lote 7: Large text, the accessibility pass, the aliases out, baselines and docs

### Task 45 (Lote 7): The large-text layout

Decision 6, the `data-letra="grande"` column of its table: the sign bar and the nav move into the flow, the nav keeps
its three icons with hidden labels, the thumb shrinks, the title caps at 1.5rem, pills wrap with the icon on top, the
gate 2 video takes the full width, the badges shrink and the toast sits at the bottom. The rules are unlayered so they
beat the `sticky` and size utilities. One rule beyond the table: a long word wraps instead of pushing the page sideways
(WCAG 1.4.10, and the spec's "no route scrolls horizontally" check).

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/e2e/ayudas.ts` (append `conLetraGrande`)
- Create: `tests/e2e/letra-grande.spec.ts`
- Modify: `app/globals.css`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Append to `tests/e2e/ayudas.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Decision 6 at the accessibility sizes: the root at 212.5 % (34 px, under 18 rem across a 390 px phone), then the head
 * script's check again, as iOS triggers it when the app comes back with a new text size.
 */
export async function conLetraGrande(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await page.addStyleTag({ content: "html { font-size: 212.5% !important }" })
  await page.evaluate(() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event("resize")))
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("data-letra", "grande")
}
```

```ts
// tests/e2e/letra-grande.spec.ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { bloquearRedExterna, configurarServicios, conLetraGrande, simularMedios } from "./ayudas"
import { sembrarEscena } from "./escena"

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: "reduce" })
  await bloquearRedExterna(page)
  await configurarServicios()
  await simularMedios(page)
})

test("at large text the nav sits after the content with three named icons and no visible labels", async ({ page }) => {
  await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto("/")
  await conLetraGrande(page)

  const nav = page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Secciones" })
  await expect(nav).toHaveCSS("position", "static")
  for (const nombre of ["Inicio", "Métricas", "Ajustes"]) {
    const pestana = nav.getByRole("link", { name: nombre })
    await expect(pestana).toBeVisible()
    expect((await pestana.boundingBox())?.height ?? 0, nombre).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44)
    expect((await nav.getByText(nombre, { exact: true }).boundingBox())?.width ?? 0, nombre).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1)
  }
})

test("at large text the sign bar sits at the end of gate 1, round all over, with the smaller thumb", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  const { guion } = await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto(`/guion/${guion}`)
  await conLetraGrande(page)

  const barra = page.locator("[data-barra-de-firma]")
  await expect(barra).toHaveCSS("position", "static")
  await expect(barra).toHaveCSS("border-bottom-left-radius", "28px")
  const raiz = await page.evaluate(() => Number.parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize))
  expect((await page.getByTestId("pulgar-firma").boundingBox())?.width ?? 0).toBeCloseTo(2.25 * raiz, 0)
  await expect(page.locator("h1")).toHaveCSS("font-size", `${1.5 * raiz}px`)
})

test("at large text the toast sits at the bottom, where nothing is pinned", async ({ page }) => {
  const { guion } = await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto(`/guion/${guion}?deshecho=1`)
  await conLetraGrande(page)

  const aviso = page.locator(".aviso")
  await expect(aviso).toContainText("Listo, no grabé nada.")
  const caja = await aviso.boundingBox()
  expect(Math.round((caja?.y ?? 0) + (caja?.height ?? 0))).toBe((page.viewportSize()?.height ?? 0) - 16)
})

test("at large text the gate 2 video takes the full width at 9:16", async ({ page }) => {
  const { video } = await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto(`/video/${video}`)
  await conLetraGrande(page)

  const caja = await page.locator(".video-puerta").boundingBox()
  expect(caja?.width ?? 0).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(340)
  expect((caja?.height ?? 0) / (caja?.width ?? 1)).toBeCloseTo(16 / 9, 1)
})

test("no screen scrolls sideways at large text on a 390 px phone", async ({ page }) => {
  const { guion, video } = await sembrarEscena()
  const desbordes: string[] = []
  for (const ruta of [
    "/",
    "/ideas",
    "/calendario",
    "/calendario?vista=agenda",
    "/calendario?vista=ritmo",
    "/metricas",
    "/ajustes",
    "/ajustes/cuentas",
    `/guion/${guion}`,
    `/video/${video}`,
    "/l",
  ]) {
    await page.goto(ruta)
    await conLetraGrande(page)
    const { ancho, vista } = await page.evaluate(() => ({
      ancho: document.documentElement.scrollWidth,
      vista: window.innerWidth,
    }))
    if (ancho > vista) desbordes.push(`${ruta}: ${ancho} > ${vista}`)
  }
  expect(desbordes).toEqual([])
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail**

Run: `pnpm test:e2e tests/e2e/letra-grande.spec.ts`
Expected: FAIL: the attribute is set (Task 4), but the nav and the bar compute `position: sticky`, the labels are 40 px
wide, the thumb is 3rem, the toast sits above the nav position and the video is 9.5rem wide.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the CSS**

Append to `app/globals.css`, after the reduced-motion block:

```css
/* ---------- Large text (decision 6): the head script sets data-letra="grande" when fewer than 18 rem fit across.
   Unlayered, so these beat the sticky and size utilities. ---------- */
html[data-letra="grande"] {
  --pulgar-firma: 2.25rem;
  --pista-firma: 3rem;
  --hueco-etiqueta: 0.6rem;
}
/* A long word wraps instead of pushing the page sideways (WCAG 1.4.10). */
html[data-letra="grande"] body {
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .barra-de-firma {
  position: static;
  margin: 24px 0 calc(16px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  padding-bottom: 14px;
  border-radius: var(--r-barra);
  box-shadow: var(--relieve);
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .nav-inferior {
  position: static;
  margin: 0 12px calc(12px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  padding-bottom: 8px;
  border-radius: var(--r-panel);
  box-shadow: var(--relieve);
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .nav-inferior a {
  min-height: 2.75rem;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .nav-inferior svg {
  width: 1.2rem;
  height: 1.2rem;
}
/* Hidden, still the accessible name of each tab (iOS's Large Content Viewer does not reach web content). */
html[data-letra="grande"] .nav-etiqueta {
  position: absolute;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  margin: -1px;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  clip-path: inset(50%);
  white-space: nowrap;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] h1.text-pantalla {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .pildora {
  align-items: flex-start;
  border-radius: 20px;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .video-puerta {
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  aspect-ratio: 9 / 16;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .insignia-video {
  font-size: 0.75rem;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .aviso[data-lugar] {
  top: auto;
  bottom: calc(16px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .aviso > span {
  flex-basis: 100%;
}
html[data-letra="grande"] .aviso > button {
  margin-left: auto;
}
```

### Task 46 (Lote 7): The bars stick, and the toast never covers them

The spec's E2E checks at the default size: the sign bar's bottom equals the viewport's while the script scrolls
(sticky works, risk "Sticky instead of fixed"), and the toast never overlaps the sign bar or the nav (bounding boxes).
Pure verification of Tasks 21, 23 and 24: the spec passes on arrival; if it does not, the fix goes back to the task that
owns the bar or the toast.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/e2e/barras.spec.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

```ts
// tests/e2e/barras.spec.ts
import { expect, type Locator, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { abrirDesplegable, bloquearRedExterna, configurarServicios, simularMedios } from "./ayudas"
import { sembrarEscena } from "./escena"

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: "reduce" })
  await bloquearRedExterna(page)
  await configurarServicios()
  await simularMedios(page)
})

async function caja(elemento: Locator) {
  const medida = await elemento.boundingBox()
  if (!medida) throw new Error("El elemento no está en pantalla")
  return medida
}

test("the sign bar stays at the bottom of the screen while the script scrolls", async ({ page }) => {
  const { guion } = await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto(`/guion/${guion}`)
  const alto = page.viewportSize()?.height ?? 0
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollHeight > window.innerHeight)).toBe(true)

  for (const y of [0, 400]) {
    await page.evaluate((destino) => window.scrollTo(0, destino), y)
    const barra = await caja(page.locator("[data-barra-de-firma]"))
    expect(Math.abs(barra.y + barra.height - alto), `scroll ${y}`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1)
  }
})

test("on a signing screen the toast sits at the top and never covers the sign bar", async ({ page }) => {
  const { guion } = await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto(`/guion/${guion}?deshecho=1`)

  const aviso = page.locator(".aviso")
  await expect(aviso).toContainText("Listo, no grabé nada.")
  const toast = await caja(aviso)
  const barra = await caja(page.locator("[data-barra-de-firma]"))
  expect(toast.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
  expect(toast.y + toast.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(barra.y)
})

test("on Inicio the toast sits above the bottom nav", async ({ page }) => {
  await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto("/")
  await abrirDesplegable(page, "Consultas")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Llegó una consulta" }).click()

  const aviso = page.locator(".aviso")
  await expect(aviso).toContainText(/^Anotada\./)
  const toast = await caja(aviso)
  const nav = await caja(page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Secciones" }))
  expect(toast.y + toast.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(nav.y)
})
```

Expected: PASS (3 tests). Then `pnpm check`.

### Task 47 (Lote 7): axe on the new states, at large text and with more contrast

Decision 7 and the spec's testing section: axe WCAG 2.2 AA on every route plus the new states (voice failure, empty
HeyGen wallet, Inicio with failures, the disconnect disclosure) and the large-text variants, and the same with
`prefers-contrast: more`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/e2e/accesibilidad.spec.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**

In `tests/e2e/accesibilidad.spec.ts`:
- replace the imports with

```ts
import AxeBuilder from "@axe-core/playwright"
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { TEXTO_SIN_VOZ } from "@/lib/produccion/voz"
import { pool } from "../integration/db"
import { ajustarPieza, sembrarIdea, sembrarPieza } from "../integration/siembra"
import {
  bloquearRedExterna,
  configurarServicios,
  conectarLinkedin,
  conLetraGrande,
  desconectarLinkedin,
  GUION_VALIDO,
  simularMedios,
  TEXTO_VALIDO,
} from "./ayudas"
import { sembrarEscena } from "./escena"
```

- replace the body of "every screen passes WCAG 2.x AA" from `const hallazgos: string[] = []` to the end with a call to
  a shared helper, and add the helper and the new tests after it:

```ts
test("every screen passes WCAG 2.x AA", async ({ page }) => {
  // Tall enough that the sticky bottom bar never covers content while axe measures target sizes (it depends on scroll).
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 2600 })
  expect(await auditar(page, await rutas())).toEqual([])
})

/** axe on each route; `antes` runs after each load (large text, an open disclosure). */
async function auditar(page: Page, lista: string[], antes?: (page: Page) => Promise<void>): Promise<string[]> {
  const hallazgos: string[] = []
  for (const ruta of lista) {
    await page.goto(ruta)
    if (antes) await antes(page)
    // The frame of the video is the user's footage: its contrast is not the app's.
    const { violations } = await new AxeBuilder({ page })
      .withTags(["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21a", "wcag21aa", "wcag22aa"])
      .exclude("video")
      .analyze()
    hallazgos.push(...violations.map((v) => `${ruta}: ${v.id} ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(" ")).join(", ")}`))
  }
  return hallazgos
}

/** The states v6 draws that the scene does not: a voice that failed, Inicio with failures. */
async function estadosNuevos(): Promise<string[]> {
  const { video } = await sembrarEscena()
  const idea = await sembrarIdea({ pilar: "deseo", gancho: "El deseo no se apaga, se esconde", estado: "usada" })
  const fallaDeVoz = await sembrarPieza("guion_por_aprobar", {
    titulo: "El deseo no se apaga, se esconde",
    idea_id: idea,
    guion: GUION_VALIDO,
    texto: TEXTO_VALIDO,
  })
  await ajustarPieza(fallaDeVoz, { audio_id: null, explicacion: TEXTO_SIN_VOZ })
  await ajustarPieza(video, { explicacion: "La hora aprobada pasó sin publicarse. Elige una nueva fecha." })
  await pool.query("delete from public.avisos")
  return ["/", `/guion/${fallaDeVoz}`]
}

test("the new states pass WCAG 2.x AA: a failed voice, an empty wallet, Inicio with failures, the disconnect", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 2600 })
  const hallazgos = await auditar(page, await estadosNuevos())

  await configurarServicios({ heygenSaldo: 0 })
  const { guion } = await sembrarEscena()
  hallazgos.push(...(await auditar(page, [`/guion/${guion}`])))

  await conectarLinkedin()
  try {
    hallazgos.push(
      ...(await auditar(page, ["/ajustes/cuentas"], async (pagina) => {
        await pagina.getByText("Desconectar LinkedIn").click()
        await expect(pagina.getByText("Tus videos dejarán de salir solos ahí")).toBeVisible()
      })),
    )
  } finally {
    await desconectarLinkedin()
  }
  expect(hallazgos).toEqual([])
})

test("every screen passes WCAG 2.x AA at large text", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 2600 })
  expect(await auditar(page, await rutas(), conLetraGrande)).toEqual([])
})

test("every screen passes WCAG 2.x AA with more contrast, and the new states too", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.emulateMedia({ contrast: "more", reducedMotion: "reduce" })
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 2600 })
  const hallazgos = await auditar(page, await rutas())
  hallazgos.push(...(await auditar(page, await estadosNuevos())))
  expect(hallazgos).toEqual([])
})

test("more contrast outlines the raised controls and turns the quiet text to ink", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.emulateMedia({ contrast: "more", reducedMotion: "reduce" })
  await sembrarEscena()
  await page.goto("/ajustes")
  await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "Cuentas conectadas" })).toBeVisible()
  // Computed colours, not the custom property: the contrast block declares --apagado as var(--tinta).
  const { contorno, colorApagado, colorTinta } = await page.evaluate(() => {
    const control = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".shadow-relieve")
    const muestra = document.createElement("span")
    muestra.className = "text-apagado"
    const referencia = document.createElement("span")
    referencia.className = "text-tinta"
    document.body.append(muestra, referencia)
    return {
      contorno: control ? getComputedStyle(control).outlineStyle : "sin control",
      colorApagado: getComputedStyle(muestra).color,
      colorTinta: getComputedStyle(referencia).color,
    }
  })
  expect(contorno).toBe("solid")
  expect(colorApagado).toBe(colorTinta)
})
```

```ts
  await capturaDe(page, "guion-escuchada-letra-grande")
})

test("guion sin escuchar", async ({ page }) => {
  const id = await guionSembrado("guion_por_aprobar")
  const fila = await leerFila(id)
  await ajustarPieza(id, {
    audio_id: claveDeToma(id, huellaDelGuion(fila.guion, leerConfigProduccion(CONFIG).elevenlabs), fila.intento),
    explicacion: null,
    avatar_id: "look-a",
  })
  await page.goto(`/guion/${id}`)
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Escuchar la voz" })).toBeVisible()
  await capturaDe(page, "guion-sin-escuchar")
})

test("guion preparando voz", async ({ page }) => {
  await vaciarPiezasEIdeas()
  const idea = await sembrarIdea({ pilar: "deseo", gancho: "El deseo no se apaga, se esconde" })
  // With tone marks, as v6 3 draws them in the script she reads while the voice is made.
  const id = await sembrarPieza("grabando_voz", {
    titulo: "El deseo no se apaga, se esconde",
    idea_id: idea,
    guion: `[warm] ${GUION_VALIDO.replace(". ", ". [pause] ")}`,
    texto: TEXTO_VALIDO,
  })
  await page.goto(`/guion/${id}`)
  await expect(page.getByText("[warm]")).toBeVisible()
  await capturaDe(page, "guion-preparando-voz")
})

test("guion falla voz", async ({ page }) => {
  const id = await guionSembrado("guion_por_aprobar")
  await ajustarPieza(id, { audio_id: null, explicacion: TEXTO_SIN_VOZ })
  await page.goto(`/guion/${id}`)
  await expect(page.getByText("No pude preparar tu voz.")).toBeVisible()
  await capturaDe(page, "guion-falla-voz")
})

test("guion sin saldo", async ({ page }) => {
  await configurarServicios({ heygenSaldo: 0 })
  const id = await guionSembrado("guion_por_aprobar")
  await vozEscuchada(page, id)
  await page.goto(`/guion/${id}`)
  await expect(page.getByText(/^Tu saldo de API de HeyGen no alcanza/)).toBeVisible()
  await capturaDe(page, "guion-sin-saldo")
})

test("guion firmado ventana", async ({ page }) => {
  const id = await guionSembrado("en_render")
  const cliente = await pool.connect()
  try {
    await cliente.query("begin")
    await cliente.query("set local session_replication_role = replica")
    await cliente.query("update public.piezas set puerta1_en = now(), explicacion = null where id = $1", [id])
    await cliente.query("commit")
  } finally {
    cliente.release()
  }
  const { version } = await leerFila(id)
  await page.clock.install()
  await page.goto(`/guion/${id}?firmada=${version}`)
  await expect(page.getByText("Firmaste")).toBeVisible()
  // The 8 s window would close before a slow capture ends: the page clock stops, the seconds are masked.
  await page.clock.pauseAt(new Date(Date.now() + 1000))
  await capturaDe(page, "guion-firmado-ventana", [page.getByText(/^Grabo tu video en/)])
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The comparison sheet on v6**

```ts
// scripts/hoja-de-comparacion.ts
// Puts each app capture next to its Soft UI v6 render in ONE self-contained HTML (images inline), for the review of
// the migration against the design. Captures without masks, at 390 css px:
//   VISUAL_FINALES=<carpeta> pnpm test:e2e tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts
//   VISUAL_FINALES=<carpeta> pnpm comparar [salida.html]
// Without VISUAL_FINALES it reads the committed baselines (masked dates).
import { existsSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { homedir } from "node:os"
import { join } from "node:path"

const FINALES = process.env.VISUAL_FINALES
const CAPTURAS = FINALES ?? join(process.cwd(), "tests", "e2e", "visual.spec.ts-snapshots")
const SUFIJO = FINALES ? ".png" : "-app-darwin.png"
const V6 = join(homedir(), "Projects", "conidolly-rediseno", "soft-ui", "v6")
const SALIDA = process.argv[2] ?? join(homedir(), "Projects", "conidolly-rediseno", "comparacion-soft-ui.html")

interface Seccion {
  titulo: string
  /** The app captures, by snapshot name, left to right. */
  capturas: { nombre: string; rotulo: string }[]
  /** The v6 renders, by file name, left to right; empty where v6 has no board for the screen. */
  maquetas: string[]
  nota?: string
}

const SIN_LAMINA = "v6 no dibuja esta pantalla: se revisa contra la gramática (decisión 14)."

const SECCIONES: Seccion[] = [
  {
    titulo: "1. Inicio",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "inicio", rotulo: "Con piezas" },
      { nombre: "inicio-letra-grande", rotulo: "Letra grande" },
    ],
    maquetas: ["1-inicio-completa.png", "1-inicio-200-completa.png"],
  },
  { titulo: "Inicio vacío", capturas: [{ nombre: "inicio-vacio", rotulo: "Sin firmas" }], maquetas: [], nota: SIN_LAMINA },
  {
    titulo: "8. Inicio con fallas",
    capturas: [{ nombre: "inicio-con-fallas", rotulo: "Una falla y una hora vencida" }],
    maquetas: ["8-inicio-con-fallas-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "2. Puerta 1, lista para firmar",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "guion-escuchada", rotulo: "Voz escuchada" },
      { nombre: "guion-escuchada-letra-grande", rotulo: "Letra grande" },
    ],
    maquetas: ["2-guion-completa.png", "2-guion-200-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "2b. Puerta 1, sin escuchar",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "guion-sin-escuchar", rotulo: "Avatar elegido, voz sin escuchar" },
      { nombre: "guion-con-voz", rotulo: "Voz lista, sin avatar" },
      { nombre: "guion-por-aprobar", rotulo: "La escena" },
    ],
    maquetas: ["2b-guion-sin-escuchar-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "3. Preparando la voz",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "guion-preparando-voz", rotulo: "Con marcas de tono" },
      { nombre: "guion-grabando", rotulo: "Sin marcas" },
    ],
    maquetas: ["3-guion-preparando-voz-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "4. Firmado, dentro de la ventana",
    capturas: [{ nombre: "guion-firmado-ventana", rotulo: "Deshacer en la página (segundos ocultos)" }],
    maquetas: ["4-grabando-video-completa.png", "4b-grabando-video-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "7. La voz falló",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "guion-falla-voz", rotulo: "Con su razón" },
      { nombre: "guion-sin-voz", rotulo: "Sin razón" },
    ],
    maquetas: ["7-falla-voz-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "8b. El video falló",
    capturas: [{ nombre: "guion-error", rotulo: "Error de producción" }],
    maquetas: ["8b-falla-grabacion-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "9. Sin saldo de HeyGen",
    capturas: [{ nombre: "guion-sin-saldo", rotulo: "La firma queda cerrada" }],
    maquetas: ["9-guion-sin-saldo-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "5. Puerta 2, video visto",
    capturas: [{ nombre: "video-por-aprobar", rotulo: "Lista para firmar" }],
    maquetas: ["5-revision-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "5b. Puerta 2, video sin terminar",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "video-sin-terminar", rotulo: "Al abrir" },
      { nombre: "video-sin-terminar-letra-grande", rotulo: "Letra grande" },
    ],
    maquetas: ["5b-revision-sin-terminar-completa.png", "5b-revision-sin-terminar-200-completa.png"],
  },
  {
    titulo: "6. Cuentas",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "ajustes-cuentas", rotulo: "Cuentas" },
      { nombre: "ajustes-cuentas-letra-grande", rotulo: "Letra grande" },
    ],
    maquetas: ["6-cuentas-completa.png", "6-cuentas-200-completa.png"],
    nota: "Sin el saldo por cuenta de la lámina: queda para la especificación de lógica (pendiente h).",
  },
  {
    titulo: "6b. Desconectar LinkedIn",
    capturas: [{ nombre: "cuentas-desconectar", rotulo: "Confirmación abierta" }],
    maquetas: ["6b-cuentas-desconectar-completa.png"],
  },
  { titulo: "Ajustes", capturas: [{ nombre: "ajustes", rotulo: "Ajustes" }], maquetas: [], nota: SIN_LAMINA },
  { titulo: "Ideas", capturas: [{ nombre: "ideas", rotulo: "Mazo" }], maquetas: [], nota: SIN_LAMINA },
  {
    titulo: "Calendario",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "calendario-mes", rotulo: "Mes" },
      { nombre: "calendario-agenda", rotulo: "Agenda" },
      { nombre: "calendario-ritmo", rotulo: "Ritmo" },
    ],
    maquetas: [],
    nota: SIN_LAMINA,
  },
  { titulo: "Métricas", capturas: [{ nombre: "metricas", rotulo: "Con tres reels" }], maquetas: [], nota: SIN_LAMINA },
  {
    titulo: "Ingreso",
    capturas: [
      { nombre: "ingreso", rotulo: "Paso inicial" },
      { nombre: "ingreso-codigo-error", rotulo: "Código equivocado" },
    ],
    maquetas: [],
    nota: SIN_LAMINA,
  },
  {
    titulo: "Página pública /l",
    capturas: [{ nombre: "l", rotulo: "Enlace de la bio" }],
    maquetas: [],
    nota: "Frambuesa de borde a borde (decisión 16): se compara con la sección 2 del brandkit.",
  },
]

const aDatos = (ruta: string) => `data:image/png;base64,${readFileSync(ruta).toString("base64")}`

function figura(ruta: string, rotulo: string): string {
  if (!existsSync(ruta))
    return `<figure><figcaption>${rotulo}</figcaption><p class="falta">Falta la captura: ${ruta}</p></figure>`
  return `<figure><figcaption>${rotulo}</figcaption><img src="${aDatos(ruta)}" alt="${rotulo}" /></figure>`
}

function seccionHtml(seccion: Seccion): string {
  const izquierda = seccion.capturas.map((c) => figura(join(CAPTURAS, `${c.nombre}${SUFIJO}`), c.rotulo)).join("")
  const derecha =
    seccion.maquetas.length > 0
      ? seccion.maquetas.map((archivo) => figura(join(V6, archivo), archivo)).join("")
      : '<figure><figcaption>Soft UI v6</figcaption><p class="falta">Sin lámina para esta pantalla.</p></figure>'
  return `<section>
  <h2>${seccion.titulo}</h2>
  ${seccion.nota ? `<p class="nota">${seccion.nota}</p>` : ""}
  <div class="par"><div class="lado"><h3>Aplicación</h3><div class="fila">${izquierda}</div></div><div class="lado"><h3>Soft UI v6</h3><div class="fila">${derecha}</div></div></div>
</section>`
}

const html = `<!doctype html>
<html lang="es-CL">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
<title>Soft UI v6: la app junto a las láminas</title>
<style>
  :root { --piedra: #ebebea; --luz: #fbfbfa; --tinta: #262626; --apagado: #5a5a59; --hilo: #dcdcdb; }
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  body { margin: 0; padding: 32px 24px 96px; background: var(--piedra); color: var(--tinta); font: 16px/1.5 system-ui, sans-serif; }
  h1 { font-size: 28px; margin: 0 0 8px; }
  p.intro { max-width: 70ch; color: var(--apagado); margin: 0 0 40px; }
  section { border-top: 1px solid var(--tinta); padding-top: 16px; margin-top: 48px; }
  h2 { font-size: 20px; margin: 0 0 4px; }
  h3 { font-size: 13px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .04em; color: var(--apagado); margin: 16px 0 8px; }
  .nota { margin: 0 0 8px; color: var(--apagado); max-width: 80ch; }
  .par { display: flex; gap: 48px; align-items: flex-start; flex-wrap: wrap; }
  .fila { display: flex; gap: 24px; align-items: flex-start; }
  figure { margin: 0; }
  figcaption { font-size: 13px; color: var(--apagado); margin-bottom: 6px; }
  img { display: block; width: 390px; max-width: none; height: auto; border: 1px solid var(--hilo); background: var(--luz); }
  .falta { width: 390px; padding: 24px; border: 1px dashed var(--hilo); color: var(--apagado); }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<h1>Soft UI v6: la app junto a las láminas</h1>
<p class="intro">Cada estado tal como lo dibuja la app (390 px de ancho, página completa) a la izquierda, y el render de Soft UI v6 a la derecha, con su versión de letra grande cuando existe. El contenido de la captura es de prueba.</p>
${SECCIONES.map(seccionHtml).join("\n")}
</body>
</html>
`

writeFileSync(SALIDA, html)
console.log(`hoja: ${SALIDA} (${(html.length / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB)`)
```

### Task 52 (Lote 7): impeccable critique and audit on the local app

The exit check of the methodology (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-impeccable-metodologia.md`, table 1.1 and
phase step 4): `critique` at 34/40 or more with no P0 or P1, and `audit` without critical or high findings, on the
real app of this branch, never on production. Accepted false positives go to `.impeccable/critique/ignore.md` with
their reason.

**Files:** none (the reports land in `.impeccable/critique/` and `.impeccable/audit/`, untracked).

- [ ] **Step 1: The captures, all 31 states**

Run: `VISUAL_FINALES=$HOME/Projects/conidolly-rediseno/capturas-soft-ui pnpm test:e2e tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts`
These are the built app of this branch with the fake services (every v6 state, default and large text).

- [ ] **Step 2: The live app, on the local stack**

1. `pnpm db:start`, then start the dev server in tmux (the session hook asks for it):
   `tmux new-session -d -s dev "pnpm dev"`.
2. Proof that it is local: open `http://localhost:3000/ingreso`, ask for the code for `coni@estudio.test`, and read it
   in the local Mailpit (its address is `MAILPIT_URL` in `supabase status -o json`). If the code does not arrive
   there, or the app answers "Solo para Coni", stop and ask Federico: the dev server is not on the local stack.
3. Seed the scene: `pnpm exec tsx -e 'import("./tests/e2e/escena.ts").then((m) => m.sembrarEscena()).then((ids) => { console.log(ids); process.exit(0) })'`
   (prints the gate 1 and gate 2 piece ids).

- [ ] **Step 3: Critique and audit**

Run `/impeccable critique` on: the live routes `/`, `/guion/<guion id>`, `/video/<video id>`, `/ajustes/cuentas`,
`/ideas`, `/calendario` at 390 px (sign gate 1 by drag and by two taps, undo inside the window, open the disclosures),
plus the 31 captures of Step 1 for the states the live scene does not reach. Then `/impeccable audit` on the same
routes.

Expected: critique 34/40 or more, no P0 and no P1; audit without critical or high findings. A finding over the bar is
fixed in the task that owns its screen (failing test first, its baseline updated), and both commands run again.

- [ ] **Step 4: Clean up**

`tmux kill-session -t dev`, then `pnpm db:reset` so `pnpm test:db` finds a clean database.

### Task 53 (Lote 7): Deploy runbook (Vercel only, each step with Federico's explicit OK)

Spec "Deploy". No migration and no Trigger.dev deploy in this branch. Every command that touches the remote or
production runs only after Federico says yes to that step in the conversation.

**Files:** `docs/TRASPASO.md` (Step 7 only).

- [ ] **Step 1: Preconditions**

- Tasks 49 and 52 are done: Federico reviewed the comparison sheet against v6, and the critique and audit passed.
- The voice in gate 1 is live in production. `main` already carries `feat/voz-en-la-puerta-1` (merge `bfc29ee`, with
  migrations `20261016000200` and `20261016000210` and new Trigger tasks), and `docs/TRASPASO.md` still lists its
  deploy under "Siguiente". A Vercel deploy of `main` ships that code too: if its runbook (Task 22 of
  `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-voz-en-la-puerta-1.md`) has not run, stop here and run that one first, with
  Federico.
- Note the current production deployment for the rollback (Federico's OK, read-only): `vercel ls --prod`, and keep
  the URL of the top one.

- [ ] **Step 2: Merge (Federico's OK)**

```bash
cd ~/Projects/estudio-coni
git checkout main
git merge --no-ff feat/soft-ui -m "Merge feat/soft-ui: the app moves to Soft UI v6 (gris neutro, relief means touch, one sign control)

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
pnpm test:run && pnpm type && pnpm build
```

Expected: PASS, clean, compiles.

- [ ] **Step 3: Push (Federico's OK)**

```bash
git push origin main
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Deploy (Federico's OK)**

```bash
vercel deploy --prod --yes
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Confirm it is served** (the alias lags 15 to 45 s; repeat until it matches)

```bash
css=$(curl -s "https://conidolly.vercel.app/ingreso?v=$(date +%s)" | grep -o '/_next/static/[^"]*\.css' | head -1)
curl -s "https://conidolly.vercel.app${css}" | grep -c -- '--piedra-honda'
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' "https://conidolly.vercel.app/l?v=$(date +%s)"
```

Expected: a count of 1 or more, and `200`. Then Federico opens `/`, `/guion/<id of a piece at gate 1>` and `/l`
signed in on his phone; Coni's installed app may need to be closed and reopened to load the new CSS.

- [ ] **Step 6: Smoke on the iPhone (Federico, with Coni's installed app or his own)**

1. Dynamic Type at the default size, at xxLarge and at AX2 (Settings, Accessibility, Display and Text Size, Larger
   Text): at default the sign bar and the nav are pinned; at AX2 they sit at the end of the page, the nav shows three
   icons only, nothing scrolls sideways.
2. VoiceOver on gate 1: the track reads "Desliza para firmar"; a double tap arms it ("Toca otra vez para firmar"), a
   second double tap signs. Signing starts a real render after 8 s: tap "Deshacer" inside the window (nothing is
   charged), unless Federico chose a piece to record for real.
3. Daylight: the raised controls are still readable against the flat page outdoors (shape, text and icon carry them).
4. macOS Safari: the root stays 17 px (Web Inspector, computed `font-size` of `html`).

- [ ] **Step 7: Close the handoff (Federico's OK for the commit and the push)**

In `docs/TRASPASO.md`, in the Soft UI bullet, replace `rama `feat/soft-ui`, sin desplegar` with
`en producción desde <fecha del deploy>, merge <hash corto del merge>`, and remove the Soft UI bullet of "Siguiente".
Then:

```bash
git add docs/TRASPASO.md
git commit -m "Record the Soft UI deploy in the handoff

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
git push origin main
```

**Rollback** (Federico's OK): `vercel promote <the URL noted in Step 1>`. No data depends on the UI, so nothing else
is undone.

## Notes for the reviewer: where the code differed from the spec, and what this plan chose

1. **`data-barra` is taken.** `components/barra-de-metrica.tsx` already uses `data-barra`, so the sign bar uses
   `data-barra-de-firma` (spec: `data-barra`). The `main:has(...)` rule, `capturaDe` and the tests follow it.
2. **The home-screen icons move in lote 1, not lote 6.** `lib/diseno/marca.ts` changes in Task 3 and `app/icon.tsx`
   and `app/apple-icon.tsx` read it, so they move together.
3. **`aria-valuetext` "Sin firmar"** for the resting track is not in the spec's copy table; the slider needs a value
   text at rest, and this plan uses it.
4. **Singulars.** "revisar 1 aviso", "1 nueva", and no count when there are none: the copy table gives only plurals.
5. **Dates.** The short date reads "Sale el martes 20 · 09:00" (`etiquetaDeFecha` with `corta`; v6 uses a comma) and
   the long one joins with " · ".
6. **Destinations** read "Instagram y LinkedIn · hora de Chile" in the canonical order of `nombreDeDestinos`; the spec
   example says "LinkedIn e Instagram".
7. **The caption** shows only in "Así sale en Instagram", not again under "Más opciones" of gate 2.
8. **"Editar el texto para LinkedIn"** keeps today's label (the shorter v6 label is ambiguous next to the caption).
9. **The LinkedIn "source line"** of v6 is not drawn: no data backs it (follow-up list).
10. **Disabled thumb.** The spec draws it apagado with an arrow; this plan follows v6 (a lock icon on a flat thumb),
    which says why it is closed without colour.
11. **`--alto-nav`** follows the spec (3.5rem + 32 px + safe area), so the nav's end padding is 24 px + safe area.
12. **A scheduled piece on Inicio** links to `/video/<id>` (the spec links the rows being worked on; "programado" had
    no link).
13. **`ElegirAvatar` without looks** renders nothing on the page; the reason lives only in the sign bar's line.
14. **A finding's marked phrase** is an inline link; `data-en-linea` exempts it from the 44 px target check, as WCAG
    2.5.8 exempts inline targets.
15. **Two captures while the voice is made.** `guion-grabando` stays (golden script) and the new
    `guion-preparando-voz` carries tone marks, as v6 3 does.
16. **Cuentas "Más opciones"** opens by default when the key was refused (it holds the one thing to fix).
17. **The toast portal** renders nothing on the server (it mounts empty and fills, so it is announced).
18. **`--radius-video`** is added to `@theme` (22 px, v6); the spec's token list omits it.
19. **The working dot's halo** uses `color-mix` with `currentColor`, so it follows the ink without a new token.
20. **The price is part of the accessible name** of "Preparar mi voz US$ 0,33" (`Boton` joins it with a space).
21. **Large text, one rule beyond the table:** `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on the body at `data-letra="grande"`, so the
    spec's "no route scrolls horizontally" holds for long words (the `/l` name at 2.75rem).
22. **`video-por-aprobar` changes meaning.** It now captures the watched video (v6 5); the unwatched one is the new
    `video-sin-terminar` (5b), so the 31 captures map one to one onto v6.
23. **"Vercel only" holds only once the voice is live.** `main` carries the voice in gate 1 (merge `bfc29ee`, with
    migrations and Trigger tasks) and `docs/TRASPASO.md` still lists its deploy as next: Task 53 stops until that
    runbook ran.
24. **The critique needs the dev server in tmux** (a session hook blocks a bare dev command) and a proof that it talks
    to the local stack (the sign-in code arrives in the local Mailpit), Task 52.
25. **Answered questions** are applied as given: no side rule on the review panel (Task 13), toast at the top on
    signing screens (Task 23), "Cambiar la llave" under "Más opciones" (Task 38), "Claude (Anthropic)" kept (Task 38).
